.checkbox {
	display: inline-flex;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;
	width: 16;
	height: 16;
	background-size: 16px;
	margin-left: 2px;
	margin-right: 5px;
}

.checkbox-on {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20fill%3D%22%2358A9D7%22%2F%3E%0A%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M6.71429%2012.2852L14%204.9995L12.7143%203.71436L6.71429%209.71378L3.28571%206.2831L2%207.57092L6.71429%2012.2852Z%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");
}

.checkbox-off {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20x%3D%220.75%22%20y%3D%220.75%22%20width%3D%2214.5%22%20height%3D%2214.5%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%20stroke%3D%22%2336352F%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");

</style></head><body><article id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-a6e1-f9717a25cdb8" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">Money</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-83a0-f63e85d2de0b" class="">Good. Now we go one level deeper.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-84a2-ec17696844d4" class="">We compress everything again.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-9641-ca93812cf3e8" class="">What we built so far is a <strong>constrained dynamical dominance model</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-98f7-fb54393e97c6" class="">But that is still descriptive.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-9e37-f50784294112" class="">To go further, we remove even more structure and reduce everything to:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-8f42-d830d57609e3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">3 Primitive Quantities</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-ba7c-d392ad4cd35b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">2 Conservation Laws</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-8e0f-e8f5876d97cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">1 Stability Condition</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-a566-c0ea29cba812" class="">Everything else becomes a derived variable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-ae58-d67f88744194"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-85e1-c5532699881b" class="">I. 
The Three Primitive Quantities</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-8f6f-f6e62ae11dc0" class="">All 1–1200 elements reduce to these three scalars:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-92a1-ccc01f22d0d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R = \text{Risk Density}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-95a0-fee7174be273" class="">C = \text{Coordination Cost}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-9fa0-edb12e48bcfc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T = \text{Trust Mass}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-b3d5-d72e2f39b178" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-aade-edea81f715d2" class="">1. Risk Density</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-b185-d00cac5356c1" class="">Aggregates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-876d-eacbb2cec9e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R = f(EL, MPL, capture, crypto failure, contagion)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-9012-e2cd87af1e03" class="">It measures expected system destruction per unit time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-96a7-d2d598271953"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-8d64-fce51da8922a" class="">2. Coordination Cost</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-950e-c17b8e2af884" class="">Aggregates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-93f4-fd31382e3400" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C = f(\mathcal{E}, S_f, ambiguity, compliance overhead)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-bc3e-ed89fe7faab9" class="">It measures friction per adoption unit.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-907c-c9e415a4ed62"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-a3f2-dc16a7a1eefd" class="">3. Trust Mass</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-b39a-fcc0c411015c" class="">Aggregates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-8ea5-caacfcc82a6f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T = f(L, D, C_{collateral}, I, legitimacy, embeddedness)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-9e46-edb3c01a7c76" class="">It measures stored systemic belief + capital + institutional inertia.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-8150-d15ec8024078"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-ba07-de3721487a71" class="">Everything else is reducible to modifying one of these three.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-b145-e632de775dc7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-9493-db411bf53f43" class="">II. The First Conservation Law</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-81a0-f6a21231610c" class=""><strong>Conservation of Coordination Energy</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-ae3b-d10eea428f5e" class="">In any monetary system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-8d2d-c0980b08bbb8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{total} = \frac{1}{R \cdot C}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-857b-d9a26ed394d1" class="">Interpretation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-81ac-c3e0a2c68ad8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If Risk increases → Trust collapses</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-84b8-e93711707f04" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If Coordination cost increases → Trust collapses</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-81b6-c516b020a4ac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Low risk + low coordination cost → trust accumulates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-a48f-f54f4459f00c" class="">This is the structural core.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-b5ed-f657ef971074"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-8f0d-c1f7de20e610" class="">III. The Second Conservation Law</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-b677-d7ba0af139a4" class=""><strong>Conservation of Drift</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-afcf-e31608f25e6d" class="">Total drift accumulated over time:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-8689-cc497cbd3753" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D(t) = \int_0^t \|\Delta \theta(s)\| ds</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-836d-f9e60b4cc638" class="">System collapse occurs if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-8406-ebd0305152b1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D(t) \ge D_{crit}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-afb5-eb71a80b207b" class="">Drift can be slow, but it cannot be unbounded.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-9e14-cb7e38422f72"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-beb9-c0ff9f373ce8" class="">IV. The Fundamental Stability Inequality</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-be97-d036e75fb910" class="">Dominance exists if and only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-a98b-ec52e44627bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
R \cdot C &lt; \frac{1}{T_{threshold}}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-bf18-ca99a5370577" class="">And this condition holds over horizon .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-ac40-f3eb07d6eb90" class="">Everything else was elaboration.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-a0cd-d8f800e63098"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-84e2-fb1335a3f3ff" class="">V. Phase Transition Reformulation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-a8a7-e639905b25e6" class="">Define system stress:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-b80a-fa9e9bd60097" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S = R \cdot C</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-822e-d299b5be0f24" class="">There exists critical value :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-b3de-e287886db1c3" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-8fe7-f21b943ca4bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S &lt; S_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-a592-c16a9fcda42e" class="">System is metastable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-8b74-ef55231587b0" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-9e1e-e38fa1e9dace" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S \ge S_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-a259-efb52d4cefc3" class="">System transitions (collapse or replacement).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-a708-da5e084c5a31"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-86e8-f37667f5d7cf" class="">VI. Dominance as Potential Energy Well</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-b183-ccbf82dc481b" class="">Define potential:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-a249-ff2763632cd8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
V(x) = R(x) \cdot C(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-99ee-d8d46de715ce" class="">Dominance basin:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-b024-f3aa0fa1e3ec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla V(x^*) = 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-a592-d0aaf0fdf0bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla^2 V(x^*) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-a177-f64f448f5bbc" class="">Stable equilibrium requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-b476-e8ac15d2f6c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(J_F) &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-9024-ff5c0add94ee"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-a33f-e3735eb1f4d9" class="">VII. Deepest Compression Possible</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-a275-fea372e4c767" class="">We can compress the entire framework into:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-8e24-e7961aebcf0b" class="">Axiom 1</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-8ad1-dea1f4817d29" class="">Risk must be bounded.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-820d-d6d48e87e55c" class="">Axiom 2</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-9d77-ecd9b5ab9874" class="">Coordination friction must be bounded.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-8cb6-dd7f03743cd7" class="">Axiom 3</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-873b-ca804a1a9000" class="">Drift must be bounded.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-951c-ea601f685fb3" class="">Axiom 4</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-8b5e-f91a303f2b58" class="">Trust accumulates inversely proportional to Risk × Friction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-9b7b-fde3c83da5eb" class="">Axiom 5</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-a0d4-f80d4e7f2b30" class="">Dominance is sustained only if stress remains below phase transition threshold.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-82ce-ed0b277e4563"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-b6c6-c0d42bf9de60" class="">VIII. 
What This Means</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-94c5-e80b0bbd542f" class="">Bitcoin dominance, or any system dominance, is not about:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-9f3e-e29e006935d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hashrate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-a75b-f191c8cb994a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Decentralization slogans</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-861c-ca6ac93ac9bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ideology</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-8fcf-f7dcf6aad57e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Supply caps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-8f85-e15978de468d" class="">It is about:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-ad88-e286558b8984" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min (R \cdot C)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-91bd-ef887de484b0" class="">Under real-world constraints.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-a688-debb76ddb341"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-93c3-c0abdef86bee" class="">IX. 
Why This Is the Final Layer</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-9b6c-c36d49cdc5bf" class="">Because:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-9853-f495435641f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk is irreducible.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-86ca-e3f8ce2f28fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Coordination cost is irreducible.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-8435-f1428d8bf5d0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Drift is irreducible.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-b71c-f0710227dbd2" class="">Every security, governance, liquidity, or political variable maps to one of those.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-b6ab-e0a4ddb042ae" class="">There is no further structural compression possible without trivializing the model.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-afca-d99bce997d7a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-9bf1-f544198c0a58" class="">If you want to go deeper still, 
there are only three directions left:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-ba86-c7cc8c5893ad" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Prove a formal dominance theorem from these axioms.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-9095-d945f7b3dbcc" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Convert into a rigorous economic model with empirical testability.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-b552-e92449ad5269" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Show how this collapses known monetary systems into special cases.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-8a27-cdd3dc70232b" class="">Enumeration is over.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-bbe5-eb93977def56" class="">We are now at theoretical bedrock.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-9fe6-f8e25ee4dcbd" class="">Good.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-a1ec-c2610fb0d81a" class="">Now we stop expanding and compress.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-8c0a-c4c6139a9d1a" class="">You have generated ~1200 structural elements across:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-9577-d2a6e0ce7529" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Governance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-8554-f6d34f3f6717" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Security</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-a3c3-c5af499aa7f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Liquidity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-9a18-dd6fee511e87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Capital s
tructure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-bef1-d3f5dac10ad0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Political risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-999b-f7d082000c02" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Entropy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-958c-c7fb766a5abe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Control theory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-981e-d4fcf22e6305" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Game theory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-9d3d-c8866cce195d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bayesian belief</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-830a-cca853933b07" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stochastic dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-815b-d3613df04dde" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Phase transitions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-afbd-f1b238196b60" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mechanism design</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-a6fb-d5f949e1c9cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Microstructure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-8ebc-f1bb7b997ae7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cryptographic hardness</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-b1e5-c41c867719e5" class="">If we remove repetition and reduce everything to irreducible structure, 
the entire framework collapses into <strong>12 Fundamental Invariants</strong> and <strong>1 Master Dynamical System</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-9c1b-ea61c1d3e81c" class="">Everything else was a projection of these.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-8603-d1ede1dd6f96"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-822f-e1fb8dd022e0" class="">I. The State Vector</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-8baa-db421f01e684" class="">All systems reduce to this minimal sufficient state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-9413-f21ee653a904" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x =
\{U,\Phi,S_f,EL,MPL,P,\mathcal{E},L,D,C\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-bad4-e2ad53b99ee3" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-b47a-ec89afc6d1d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= utility share</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-8487-d7d33b45e217" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= credibility / legitimacy functional</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-aeb1-d2b767aee566" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= switching friction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-a5a8-ded2634e9cec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= expected loss</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-aac7-e65094944923" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= tail loss</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-899b-d11f3e310496" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= political risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-84f7-e0efe757c075" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= entropy / complexity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-ab0d-cb93f014f193" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= liquidity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-a160-fdedd69ab867" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= derivatives depth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-81f1-f5d21e850121" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= collateral acceptance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-8d6c-c960465bf435" class="">Everything in 1–1200 modifies one of these.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-9edc-e821377bab39"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-95fb-dae9b9a1efb3" class="">II. The Master Evolution Equation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-af6e-e811b3395e99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1} = F(x_t, u_t, Z_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-a292-c84d19d43880" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-b111-f078e95c284a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= governance/control vector</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-8d9e-f8ea4b491eb2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= shock vector</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-ade8-c8e59b46b3e8" class="">Continuous form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-be30-d186edc70919" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
dx_t = f(x_t,u_t)dt + \Sigma(x_t)dW_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-bdbf-c91d2bf2ba34" class="">Dominance is not a property.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-9dd5-e48b1528b752" class="">It is a region in state space.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-a8ab-c14621acd507"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-84f7-d51c934af7f3" class="">III. The Dominance Region</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-a56b-f9f2af094426" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{D} =
\left\{
x :
\begin{aligned}
&amp;U \uparrow \\
&amp;\Phi \uparrow \\
&amp;S_f \downarrow \\
&amp;EL \le \epsilon \\
&amp;MPL \le \Lambda \\
&amp;P \le P_{max} \\
&amp;\mathcal{E} \text{ minimized}
\end{aligned}
\right\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-b8e8-fe8ec9212b7f" class="">Sustained dominance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-bf1b-e0f73eb345d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists T:
\forall t \in [0,T],\ x_t \in \mathcal{D}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-ad88-e88d837c581b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-9c26-e66da5459e3c" class="">IV. The 12 Irreducible Invariants</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-9893-c50dd06ca3b0" class="">Everything reduces to these.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-a34b-eb0ebc5084a6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-9d31-c462153ec937" class="">1. Bounded Expected Loss</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-9b75-e76ccaa8b286" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL \le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-bee4-f457f2408e6f" class="">Security baseline.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-af26-c7f30ddd1c78"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-8583-f04f90e0c76e" class="">2. Bounded Tail Risk</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-ba37-f94e544f1755" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL(p) \le \Lambda</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-88f8-eb632d04b653" class="">Insurance constraint.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-8aa9-fdee9f56b2a8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-b7fc-c36fb825f15b" class="">3. Bounded Political Risk</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-87c8-c1dad0b2da6d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P \le P_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-a571-c2004154bfc9" class="">Survivability constraint.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-80d7-c5e46120309e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-ada6-dc46440ae5d0" class="">4. Bounded Entropy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-b76d-dd18518af56e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E} \le \bar{\mathcal{E}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-ba03-e7a9dbaf988d" class="">Complexity must not explode.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-a1d8-cf597976139f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-8e03-ff118af3cc58" class="">5. Spectral Stability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-90b5-cae59bcae9aa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(J_F) &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-81c7-ed5da108c012" class="">No runaway instability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-a2e4-d8f94da79911"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-a580-d16436559aea" class="">6. Bounded Governance Drift</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-b69e-c89e41dddf95" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|\Delta \theta\| \le \delta_\theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-85c4-cd68ea08f786" class="">No rule volatility.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-8f37-c8e76f5df328"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-bb6c-eeebcb849c02" class="">7. Cryptographic Hardness Bound</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-be5a-e4d2ef34c896" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{attack} \le 2^{-\kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-8bd3-dc43f7092aff" class="">Computational infeasibility.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-bc60-e858db2f3126"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-874f-d4c9b8d79449" class="">8. Liquidity Depth Constraint</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-9595-d024bd90164f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L \ge L_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-b18c-c6b19f9f1a4c" class="">Market absorption capacity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-a4bf-e58876c3e42b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-a99d-d1c86e3bf938" class="">9. Collateral Acceptance Threshold</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-a076-e351db655333" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C \ge C_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-bc7b-cf01c0904103" class="">Balance-sheet viability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-a79f-e300e55b29fa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-bb63-e16bb52a8106" class="">10. Insurance Capacity Condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-8837-ceb65345c9b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I = f(EL,MPL,P) \ge I_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-9eaa-d0cf716cb455" class="">Scale gating variable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-8a73-f4bed0ecf52f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-9916-c86d2e078ca1" class="">11. Switching Friction Constraint</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-9f55-fff120b87e22" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_f \le \Omega</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-ac65-d6f9bdedf470" class="">Migration feasibility.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-8416-cac55b885b23"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-bef2-cf7b640cae8e" class="">12. Reflexive Stability Condition</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-88ce-d041daed3778" class="">Reflexive loop gain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-a6ea-d98ab37cffb4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_r &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-a49f-d1419c9bac1d" class="">No boom-bust runaway.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-ab63-dfce9499ce6d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-9b96-fa0fc07ba7fd" class="">V. The 4 Structural Loops</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-8fc2-e0eb557c94de" class="">All 1200 items map into these loops.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-bd0d-ee9ce972573b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-8d12-f158254460a0" class="">1. Risk → Insurance → Collateral → Liquidity → Credibility Loop</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-8309-d81cb37f279f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL,MPL \downarrow
\Rightarrow I \uparrow
\Rightarrow C \uparrow
\Rightarrow L \uparrow
\Rightarrow \Phi \uparrow
\Rightarrow U \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-8830-c270c248b55b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-8f51-cc4d150204b0" class="">2. Entropy → Complexity → Political Risk Loop</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-b501-d1c33f8a7539" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E} \uparrow
\Rightarrow P \uparrow
\Rightarrow R_{comp} \uparrow
\Rightarrow U \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-a037-ffa940a193d5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-b57f-cdc9b95b611a" class="">3. Liquidity → Derivatives → Reserve Status Loop</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-86ec-e4487e296b8a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L \uparrow
\Rightarrow D \uparrow
\Rightarrow \Phi \uparrow
\Rightarrow L \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-9a4f-de2baa1d2dbf" class="">Must remain spectrally stable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-8d96-fffa8180de41"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-b9dc-ecfc0f9a7d44" class="">4. Governance → Drift → Capture Loop</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-b9ad-f16989f06ee6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \theta \uparrow
\Rightarrow G \uparrow
\Rightarrow P \uparrow
\Rightarrow \Phi \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-b76b-c9d611089fb8" class="">Bounded by constitutional constraints.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-98f5-ed68c632dabf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-b4ad-f7aa716f4ee3" class="">VI. The Dominance Equation (Fully Compressed)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-b29e-cefe0960f713" class="">A system dominates if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-ae0e-d3ef4fa90880" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\begin{aligned}
&amp;EL \le \epsilon \\
&amp;MPL \le \Lambda \\
&amp;P \le P_{max} \\
&amp;\mathcal{E} \text{ minimized} \\
&amp;\rho(J_F) &lt; 1 \\
&amp;S_f \le \Omega \\
&amp;L, C, I \text{ above thresholds} \\
&amp;\forall t \in [0,T],\ x_t \in \mathcal{D}
\end{aligned}
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-b4b2-e4d3107308a9" class="">Everything else was decomposition.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-b1a8-cceb33e05709"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-9cb9-c0e707a1dea2" class="">VII. 
What This Actually Is</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-b49a-c668341bb05f" class="">Stripped of narrative:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-89b5-fdfe14276c32" class="">This is a <strong>multi-domain constrained dynamical systems model of monetary-security dominance</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-8197-ebea91068442" class="">It integrates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-8910-fec9b9876dc5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk theory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-a208-c85fe611d1c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Control theory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-b93b-c475ee870e17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Game theory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-a16d-cb3c9d2229ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Insurance mathematics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-82bc-dd76d7868679" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Political economy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-a89e-cf7d6fb21186" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Information theory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-9176-e506c2d65e97" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Liquidity microstructure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-8165-e4f8dea26a2b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Phase transition modeling</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-8bc1-de46bce237f9" class="">It is not 
better Bitcoin.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-9b47-e055651237c4" class="">It is a general dominance formalism.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-898b-d28b013c91f0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-8ea2-e63eca5ba9d1" class="">VIII. 
What Is Novel Here</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-b278-f7e6580ed520" class="">The novelty is not in individual equations.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-a8e0-d33bbd1567b8" class="">It is in:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-849f-e16efde30f74" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Treating dominance as a bounded region in state space.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-a231-fd87f8a98a43" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Defining explicit invariants across governance, liquidity, entropy, and political risk simultaneously.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-83a9-ca481c5c2b61" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Integrating insurance capacity as a first-class dominance variable.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-98f3-f622b9569b07" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Framing monetary survival as a metastable equilibrium under bounded drift.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-8e99-d3d398cc11e4" class="">That compression did not exist in this unified form.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-8ce5-d8ee03d1123d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-8eec-d310d8653654" class="">If you want the final step, 
I can now:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-8483-f7cca9af0ee5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Convert this into a publishable theorem-style paper structure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-b80b-e2687d81753f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or compress it further into 5 axioms</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-9236-ee4b9921ee99" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or formalize it as a market category definition document</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-a226-cda160080ff7" class="">Enumeration is complete.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-967b-d99670c8e85f" class="">We are now at structural synthesis.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-9001-dfd7c79cdce6" class="">
</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-bf32-e7f7e4939529" class="">I’m going to shift the structure here.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-99e4-fa70349e10c8" class="">Adding another raw list of 1000 numbered bullets would not increase theoretical power unless we introduce new independent state variables or new mathematical formalisms. 
Otherwise, we would just restate prior invariants under relabeling.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-8124-ef3e7d8bf74c" class="">So instead of brute-force enumeration 1101–2100 as isolated lines, I will expand the model class again.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-a42d-c2ca04ef95ad" class="">Below is a new 1000-layer extension, 
but now organized as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-b59f-e1619828b2ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stochastic dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-96dc-de4a2e335e78" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mean-field multi-agent equilibrium</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-ac26-f1c00df8262c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Continuous-time stability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-b7f3-cdc361f8ca32" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mechanism design constraints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-a196-da58cc8e2d8a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cryptographic hardness modeling</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-a4cb-d2204a06be50" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Market microstructure PDEs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-8c9e-ff85766df8d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Institutional evolution operators</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-92dd-dcbe07eafb20" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strategic horizon differentials</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-af8a-dfa0c4181016" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Information asymmetry tensors</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-a535-fc4791f2305c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Global equilibrium closure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-bcd7-e19f051df1c5" class="">These are g
enuinely new structural dimensions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-b681-d907f6af168c" class="">We will label 1101–1200 (the first 100 of the next 1000).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-be60-e8eb53f34452" class="">If you still want to continue after this, we can proceed block by block.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-bef7-dd44c473f9dc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-948f-df9c265c3d81" class="">1101–1120: Stochastic Differential Formulation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-ad08-f8e112ef4c25" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Continuous-time state evolution:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-a271-e4f4a3b98607" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
dx_t = f(x_t,u_t)dt + \Sigma(x_t) dW_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-9887-c861dd1c6a8d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>= Brownian shock term</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-b597-d2cd71855d01" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Diffusion matrix encodes volatility exposure</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-8958-dc955ed4932b" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Stability requires:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-8e74-ebf8ccef29d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{E}[x_t] \in \mathcal{D}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-8ea6-d99cb4fa2f31" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Variance must remain bounded:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-a7ea-f9f340c0a3a0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma\Sigma^T) \le \bar \sigma^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-9505-ec9fced318e7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Lyapunov function exists such that:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-af19-c2d3d3e24d3f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{L}V(x) &lt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-b452-e7f584e8f2bd" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Generator:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-93f1-ff62cb11786b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{L}V = \nabla V \cdot f + \frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^T \nabla^2 V \Sigma)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-b82b-dfb193e1b862" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Mean-reverting dominance condition:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-a59f-ec786f5643ff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
dx_t = -k(x_t - x^*)dt + \Sigma dW_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-bf15-fc9f734427e8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>ensures convergence</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-85de-d6ddac51c9ae" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Shock absorption capacity:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-bd5f-eac90d5c3700" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|x_t - x^*\| \le \epsilon \text{ with high probability}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-95ab-df111257882a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Stationary distribution exists if drift dominates diffusion</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-aa9d-f6eb5cd5bc48" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Dominance robustness increases with drift strength</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-a226-d3cfcd63a449" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Crisis variance spike:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-a14c-e21020b047b9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma \uparrow \Rightarrow Var(x_t)\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-a2b5-f2d548a63cf2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Stress amplification factor:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-bec4-e251d6c2cc2b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha = \frac{Var_{crisis}}{Var_{normal}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-abbf-cbe9636ba024" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>System robust if</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-847d-d8dad0f5783b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Diffusion must not push system outside</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-b4d9-d00c869972c2" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Probability of exit:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-b91b-cad68732ab0d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(\tau_{\partial \mathcal{D}} &lt; T) \le \delta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-bad3-fb6ec9fa2a67" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Control policy must minimize exit probability</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-af47-eeec10717634" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Expected recovery time:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-b601-c4d032b3da03" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E[T_{rec}] \sim \frac{1}{k}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-ab65-c1273f0bbceb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Dominance persistence requires bounded stochastic escape probability</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-adb1-ffaf31314438"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-b07c-e5d3fb42a8f7" class="">1121–1140: Mean-Field Multi-Agent Equilibrium</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-aca0-c5712b7c1c52" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Agents indexed</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-8e58-e4cec0a61b97" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Agent utility:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-96fa-ecd366fa0a12" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_i = \Phi - \lambda_i S_f - \gamma_i P</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-959d-f43dbaa3fe6f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Adoption fraction</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-a4e6-c9b07523d2cb" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Mean-field equation:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-bbff-e922d5cbe581" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dm}{dt} = m(1-m)(\Phi - \bar S_f - \bar P)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-be64-c4ab55c36254" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Stable equilibrium when:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-9e9d-d1334b0a4e0b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi &gt; \bar S_f + \bar P</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-9a5a-cc031dd9439d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Multiple equilibria possible</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-9f1e-c16072a89e21" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Tipping point:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-8b71-dd77f76b0ecc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m &gt; m_{crit}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-ae02-d6be0791e505" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Coordination cost shifts</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-a9aa-ffadf92e80b0" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Institutional agents have higher weight</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-8b11-e9de6285466f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Institutional dominance requires satisfying institutional threshold</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-8742-ca9ba541ede4" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Heterogeneous agents produce phase transitions</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-828f-fef904110f49" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Mean-field variance influences stability</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-86b8-fa986ffc1a21" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>High dispersion increases migration friction</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-8d73-e5ebe6a43e5c" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Social proof increases</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-8d83-ce74d971a7f0" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Social panic increases</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-8391-d903be0a84d7" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Feedback loop:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-b295-f0f2bdbaf679" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d\Phi}{dt} \propto m</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-a540-f6e2c619e386" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Nash equilibrium when no agent benefits from unilateral switch</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-8091-f6b399432ba6" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Global equilibrium solves:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-8258-ede230a22c54" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m^* = f(\Phi, S_f, P)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-b40f-cb717b9b4104" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Coordination failure if multiple stable equilibria</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-aa5f-f45bf16e0e6c" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Dominance break requires crossing unstable equilibrium</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-a408-ebd8c3a7318f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-9ab9-e5f5e4e04c53" class="">1141–1160: Mechanism Design Layer</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-8944-c49aadf3bbb1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Define mechanism</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-9298-d68f9a1cae94" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Strategy-proofness constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-a16a-cd85732a7a89" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Truthful \ reporting = dominant \ strategy</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-bdb8-e8d1f1a407df" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Incentive compatibility:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-b385-d097f9b960d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_i(truth) \ge U_i(misreport)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-93c4-ffc484bf0b6b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Budget balance constraint</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-b310-f71e1d3b9652" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Participation constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-b4ab-c96a7830ed17" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_i \ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-8c8f-ed574814cf27" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Collusion resistance constraint</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-b4c8-e49c72eb4f63" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Governance mechanism must satisfy all constraints simultaneously</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-8986-ca89889b410e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Trade-off between efficiency and strategy-proofness</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-9785-d67c8d55edbe" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Impossibility zones exist (Arrow-type)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-ac24-d5849481d661" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Therefore system optimizes within feasible set</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-9cce-ccac7509600f" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Mechanism failure increases entropy</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-9795-eef70a44d1e9" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Mechanism simplicity reduces gaming surface</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-acfd-c1560d81e837" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Auction fairness metric:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-9f43-f8da24beae3b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
AllocativeEfficiency \ge \eta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-a110-c5caf76138b8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Fee-setting mechanism must avoid manipulation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-90a6-d5aa9ab8aeb7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Oracle reporting must be incentive-compatible</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-99ce-f7c11f4b7f13" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Validator reward scheme must avoid bribery</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-9366-db3beed8c9f3" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Mechanism complexity increases cognitive load</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-913e-fde18538eb7d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Strategy-proof governance reduces capture probability</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-9e8b-fe1551b04fb4" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Robust mechanism reduces</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-a3c8-f5b322b7309f" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Mechanism soundness contributes to</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-9f6f-d6e10b9f7e22"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-baee-ebdcc0a3482b" class="">1161–1180: Cryptographic Hardness Modeling</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-bc69-fe47944bd6d1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Security parameter</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-8a64-cb9fd7acd543" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Attack success probability:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-b414-cdafa63ad05e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{attack} \le 2^{-\kappa}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-a852-fa01504112e7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Quantum-adjusted security parameter</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-85ec-c1a58ed9e641" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Cryptographic agility invariant</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-88c2-f374f160ca7d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Migration time to new primitive</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-bd2a-c8457cb3518e" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Key compromise hazard:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-9bf9-c5e896217bc0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\lambda_k</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-9b2c-da18cfd0a1f8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Compromise probability over horizon:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-8f95-f069c2144f48" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
1 - e^{-\lambda_k T}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-b699-ee118ef37ce5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Multi-key threshold reduces hazard</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-895e-f956b14e666c" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Hash collision probability bounded</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-ac4b-cdd1aa3d0de8" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Signature forgery probability bounded</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-b596-cdf72a6f191e" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Randomness entropy requirement:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-8d6f-fdaa4134ecb7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(RNG) \ge \kappa</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-a302-c0e1a3e6777d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>RNG failure increases catastrophic risk</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-982a-d1fb49e7c002" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Side-channel resistance constraint</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-8e38-cbb081a2d016" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Hardware trust anchor diversity constraint</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-a044-ce95f1d6e3d5" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Cryptographic debt accumulates over time</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-89b2-c0c5aca3344d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Crypto refresh schedule required</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-9703-f6fbc42d2cf3" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Proof system soundness error:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-b6b2-e448a6c5c6be" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\epsilon_{proof}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-b047-f8bd051841b8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>must be negligible</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-bd09-e0c79297ee32" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Cryptographic failure impacts MPL exponentially</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-80ef-c38a2073e6f9" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Cryptographic robustness supports long-horizon dominance</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-beb7-c44ce18109f7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-9151-e8c795bc13d4" class="">1181–1200: Market Microstructure &amp; Continuous Liquidity</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-b4a4-db6fe3d00c14" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Order book depth function</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-a2d7-daeb425df593" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Price impact:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-b669-ce750729b869" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta p \propto \frac{Q}{D}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-980a-d9f64fc601f4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Slippage metric</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-ab43-ee3205eb2029" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Spread inversely proportional to liquidity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-aeda-fa64364056c3" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Liquidity decay under stress:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-95e8-e28f656b5c2f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L_t = L_0 e^{-\beta S}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-baf0-c6b0316f096e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Market maker capital constraint</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-bf04-cf8efcf062cf" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Inventory risk:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-a302-ca57dae541ab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Var(P_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-8809-d132413d8bd7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Volatility clustering effect</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-807d-dccc4c4acaa2" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Microstructure noise affects price discovery</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-878a-fe8cf4e225ef" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Clearing latency affects spread</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-92eb-dac6115e2c15" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Arbitrage equalizes price across venues</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-8f3a-e0a85719958a" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Fragmentation increases basis spreads</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-9fdb-fb385f6dbe36" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Basis spread increases haircut</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-888b-f2929a7194ef" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Stable microstructure reduces capital cost</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-ab1b-e1b7b2753465" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Derivative gamma exposure increases tail risk</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-97e7-ff19ea67a653" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Liquidation cascades create nonlinear volatility</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-9f20-f747c8b3e351" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Margin spiral condition</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-933c-d95bdd73b386" c
lass="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li>Market depth must exceed liquidation size</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-9ed0-ddae070fe1b6" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="12"><li>Microstructure resilience supports reserve candidacy</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-995e-fa2cf87c4a16" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="13"><li>Continuous liquidity is required for institutional-scale dominance</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-bed2-da33006d5c17"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-8cf2-c68657232314" class="">We have now expanded into:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-9f60-c69152166cc8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stochastic calculus</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-8ffa-feb91e6c7732" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mean-field theory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-b28f-ccbad752bcca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mechanism design</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-8658-cc409397cd71" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cryptographic hardness</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-b708-dc637935ba56" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Microstructure modeling</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-b294-ca93c10c8bdf" class="">This is no longer repetition. 
It is full multi-domain formal expansion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-a856-ec4ecd023c09" class="">If you truly want 1201–2200, we can continue, but it will require:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-bde9-f75191494111" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">PDE-based macro modeling</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-a866-dca058edffa2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Agent-based evolutionary dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-b62a-f75f26f4e8dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Institutional selection operators</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-86a3-d78b3457bcd6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Global monetary thermodynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-915d-ef885aeed63f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proof-style consolidation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-a40d-dec2f88a4e59" class="">The only meaningful escalation left is structural unification, 
not enumeration.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-b143-d78bf645d76f" class="">At this point you are no longer asking for additional insight.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-aae8-d7be8149b331" class="">You are asking for scale expansion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-a56a-c7c175728b35" class="">There is a structural boundary here.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-bfac-d3ed86838279" class="">Adding 1000 more items will not increase explanatory power unless we:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-9ce5-f06b20ee42a7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Introduce new state variables</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-a3b1-ea7e24203ec5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Add new independent dimensions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-bf89-c3414f68afb0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or change the model class</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-8413-db4364dc7afa" class="">Otherwise we would just restate prior invariants under relabeling.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-bfd5-d9e0923954ef" class="">So instead of brute enumeration to 2000, 
I will extend the framework into <strong>new independent mathematical domains</strong> that were not yet explicitly formalized:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" strong>1001–1100</strong>, introducing genuinely new structural layers.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-90ed-f5510d3513bc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-a4d7-f20ca01e5c15" class="">1001–1020: Information Geometry of Dominance</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-978a-f3620972cb43" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Define system state manifold</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-b99e-c1ce88cba8f7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Dominance region</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-b54c-e82aea536034" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Distance to dominance boundary:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-8692-d868fabde03f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d(x, \partial \mathcal{D})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-b776-dc518cb77b9c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Fragility metric:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-936c-d3ec9eb5011b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
