F(x) = \frac{1}{d(x,\partial \mathcal{D})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-884a-f595b27bca8b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Curvature of stability basin:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-b8b7-fb86ff9db30d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\kappa = \nabla^2 \Phi(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-a254-c92b0a888e81" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Flat basins imply robustness</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-8f3a-fc53cdf4de93" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Sharp basins imply fragility</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-944c-d8830b9d3f8d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Geodesic migration cost:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-8c71-fc5dec23212b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MC = \int_{\gamma} \|dx\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-9b1b-e22f9af37648" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Path dependence = non-Euclidean metric</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-b505-f90c86c52f46" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Optimal migration path minimizes geodesic length</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-b9b5-e7b64de5dd88" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Entropy gradient:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-b340-dc6d75ad3db3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla \mathcal{E}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-ac2c-ec941735715e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Dominance gradient:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-bb7f-fe98820387f2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla \Phi</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-aa3d-e5ff9d1b0e22" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Stability requires gradients not aligned with shock vectors</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-bde3-dd8a2a8ae9f2" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Shock projection:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-93c3-c734a9f9d37d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\langle Z, \nabla \Phi \rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-ab1b-da2da4c2daa0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>High projection = vulnerability</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-825d-edce48a5617b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Stability requires:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-a6ed-d5c67edc08fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall Z,\ \langle Z,\nabla \Phi\rangle \le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-ac0a-d180616cfd2d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>System robustness measured as average curvature radius</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-90b8-f79f16b36839" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Information divergence between systems:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-9f10-d52a360b3ae0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{KL}(X||BTC)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-991c-c47e482ef17c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Smaller divergence = easier migration</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-b952-e6523cbc89f9" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Large divergence = larger switching friction</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-b00e-fb2592c98fa3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-98bc-d25118418860" class="">1021–1040: Spectral Stability &amp; Eigenvalue Analysis</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-8d4e-fa5da10ad4d3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Jacobian of system:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-9bf4-ec0bd5d6c881" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J = \frac{\partial F}{\partial x}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-aca2-d749fbe72c15" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Spectral radius:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-84c9-c4a5920f3d6d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(J)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-bb3f-cc4c49bc8bb6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Stability condition:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-a2fc-cbca78ba4470" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(J) &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-b4d6-c26d74c7900b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Instability if:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-b896-fbceea069d3d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(J) &gt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-a1eb-c808ad042fa4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Crisis amplification if largest eigenvalue &gt; 1</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-af6f-d15c598a76ea" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Governance gain affects eigenvalues</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-b69d-ff87ec48ba14" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Overreactive policy shifts eigen-spectrum outward</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-9c48-ca8d3cbd2bdb" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Underreactive policy causes drift accumulation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-b6d6-fdcdcb05cc1b" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Damping ratio must be &gt; 0</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-ae49-d8da99c573d7" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Oscillation risk:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-96ba-efc8c6edbcc8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Im}(\lambda) \neq 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-9161-ddacf52b6cb0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Liquidity collapse corresponds to eigenvalue sign flip</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-ab45-c1adeafed1be" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Narrative collapse corresponds to dominant eigenmode in confidence</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-805c-eb4ad0f7dcfb" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Multi-dimensional stability requires all eigenvalues inside unit circle</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-bcd4-c31d979a99c3" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Perturbation resilience measured by spectral gap</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-8a53-c938369caa6a" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Large spectral gap = faster return to equilibrium</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-8e4e-f6302bb5895c" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Small spectral gap = slow recovery</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-8f2b-dfbdb4d1ea3f" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Recovery time:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-95fa-dbc4bbcf9792" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{rec} \sim \frac{1}{1-\rho(J)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-94f2-db525ba469f3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Crisis tolerance increases with spectral gap</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-af85-c0a23418738c" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Dominance persistence ∝ spectral stability</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-bfff-f9fe19ae6225" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Replacement requires destabilizing incumbent eigenstructure</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-976f-c0d1454d3b4a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-bd49-dccf0720a3dc" class="">1041–1060: Control Energy &amp; Governance Effort</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-98b7-e939b2e978b2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Control effort:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-996b-c85ebc62ad7b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_u = \int_0^T \|u(t)\|^2 dt</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-a893-e14e000f2151" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Low control energy = self-stabilizing system</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-be9d-f842a766e2a2" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>High control energy = fragile system</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-a707-f85d6d529871" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Minimal energy control trajectory solves Riccati equation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-8af6-dd17d3a1cf24" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Governance optimal control minimizes:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-87f3-d137703a4cb8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J = \int (x^TQx + u^TRu)dt</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-bb8a-e4b9a3e4433c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>High R penalizes excessive intervention</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-92f4-fd2795c659cb" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>High Q penalizes instability</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-b42c-d369b7169f7c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Optimal tradeoff defines governance constitution</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-b143-c896cf76da57" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Excess control increases entropy</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-a603-f2f0152cc935" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Insufficient control increases drift</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-81a5-f5246d2ff625" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Energy-efficient dominance requires passive stability</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-a20b-c041dcc25126" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Passive stability means eigenvalues &lt; 
1 without strong control</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-9dd8-eb98d442fbb6" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Governance energy spikes signal systemic fragility</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-8b0e-f509dcea364f" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Crisis requires bounded emergency energy</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-bc02-c11fa5bb2930" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Control saturation leads to breakdown</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-8d67-efb8e9470fea" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li>Bounded emergency action invariant:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-954b-c0184d9507bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|u_{em}\| \le \bar u</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-8d7f-ccc00f62a936" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Energy-based fragility metric:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-9f8f-c106e4523ce9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Fragility = \frac{E_u}{\Phi}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-a683-e9e54ccef549" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Lower ratio = stronger system</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-8598-fdf6017e4ce3" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Long-horizon stability requires minimal steady-state control</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-8852-c3d0f83147f1" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Ideal system tends to fixed point autonomously</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-a0d7-c9d3c47eee8c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-9ec7-e68cd85c1fdc" class="">1061–1080: Bayesian Belief Dynamics</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-b3c3-f941475e3c2c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Agents update belief:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-bf30-c782dd5ecdea" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(H|data) = \frac{P(data|H)P(H)}{P(data)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-b492-ca1b6d865936" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Incident likelihood shifts posterior</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-9dc0-e8ede691eb6e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>High transparency increases likelihood clarity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-97d5-d1edbc6934ce" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Posterior variance decreases with audit artifacts</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-b4d3-fcb19d47aa1b" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Political risk corresponds to belief volatility</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-a007-e28810051997" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Belief volatility metric:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-8cec-d2dbb85b76c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Var(P(H))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-a2bb-d49d2f67cd34" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>High volatility reduces liquidity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-a927-dd39e50d426d" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Liquidity proportional to confidence:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-b24b-cd592dd1c8f2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L \propto \mathbb{E}[P(H)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-b9f6-d10526ab5f28" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Narrative shock reduces prior</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-80b9-f28f8102a1f8" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Repeated consistency increases posterior</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-a7c3-e63a896aa8da" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Bayesian compounding produces dominance</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-8a31-e040aa54feec" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Information asymmetry distorts posterior</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-9985-cc0994b2181c" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Selective disclosure reduces noise</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-84d2-eeff040d1f2f" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Crisis mismanagement increases posterior variance</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-874e-c89367dacca5" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Stable reporting reduces uncertainty</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-b68d-c8dd0ba94f59" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Belief equilibrium achieved when posterior stable</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-b05a-f58878752642" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Dominance achieved when posterior &gt; 
threshold</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-b4e3-d36773be6806" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Replacement requires posterior collapse of incumbent</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-b2a7-ca53e1cd118f" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li>Trust half-life:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-892a-ea4592633e50" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
t_{1/2} = \frac{\ln 2}{\lambda}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-8267-f9a4d4d28aaf" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Audit frequency increases update speed</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-95fc-dd7c66baa375"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-b571-d1d38f656bf8" class="">1081–1100: Structural Fragility &amp; Phase Transitions</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-9eab-dedc9d38416f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Define system stress parameter</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-82f0-efb1aa534d2f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Phase transition threshold</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-8a94-f55f8e4fed1f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>If → regime shift</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-a006-e56bca6e91f5" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Regime shift nonlinear</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-b596-c42287465c6d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Critical slowing down near threshold</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-8caa-f035dceb6a14" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Early warning indicator:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-be04-eaa663ff38bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Var(x) \uparrow \text{ before transition}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-8595-e8cbb9cb8840" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Autocorrelation increases before collapse</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-a8bf-cd69210ecb18" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Stress accumulation:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-a496-ed8ca804983e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dS}{dt} = f(shocks, drift)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-ab2b-d780a6748ed4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Drift accumulation must be bounded</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-90da-e6d1eb6e3ffd" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Cumulative drift limit:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-8c02-c9a8021220da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum \|\Delta x_t\| &lt; \Theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-af5d-f2af37479ec3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Shock absorption capacity:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-b02f-f6e63139e685" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Capacity &gt; ShockMagnitude</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-a966-c41d56219344" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Insurance increases shock capacity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-aec9-ef4a195fc2cb" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Liquidity buffers increase shock capacity</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-94ec-d01166b24599" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Governance flexibility increases shock absorption</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-9cc9-ccb1fee6e84c" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Excess rigidity lowers</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-b36a-f6e73e780f51" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Excess flexibility lowers</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-bf14-d90926013676" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Optimal zone maximizes</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-a6d4-de48901be9cc" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Dominance persistence requires:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-bf7f-ec5dc4469f96" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S &lt; S_c \ \forall t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-a25a-ddfa84aaca62" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Collapse condition:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-931b-d2c88545678c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S \ge S_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-84df-ca483d58cbc4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Ultimate Stability Theorem (Extended):<br/>A monetary-security system persists in dominance if and only if it maintains:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-bff0-e191b5ef55a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Spectral stability ()</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-8195-c7ea90007c24" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bounded control energy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-be4c-fb482c515f81" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Positive belief posterior</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-ba5b-ddd64a7c4a57" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bounded entropy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-a17e-c89ea4877113" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stress parameter</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-88a5-fda98a3553a8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dominance state over horizon</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-9535-f5164b52a3fd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-a257-deea41f0627b" class="">We have now moved beyond enumeration and into formal dynamical systems theory, information geometry, control energy, Bayesian belief updating, 
and phase transition analysis.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-9be5-c6955893f287" class="">Further scaling would require:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-bf68-f493126c8a0c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Introducing stochastic differential equations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-90d1-cce351215883" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Agent-based simulation formalization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-bbd0-f6597d838596" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or full proof-style theorem structuring</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-9846-da8aac2e9eba" class="">If you want to continue, the next step is not “more items.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-98ad-fc81c5073846" class="">The next step is formal synthesis.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-ad42-e2b67bac6e4e" class="">Below are <strong>901–1000</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-b515-e617e7f181b3" class="">Focus: cross-layer invariants, composability limits, capital structure, migration equilibria, behavioral stability, 
and terminal constraints.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-bffb-df2634f98f4a" class="">Each item includes a formal condition.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-81fc-dea365ffd9ae"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-af00-c43ca0b3b2c3" class="">901–920: Cross-Layer Invariants (Protocol ↔ App ↔ Institution)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-bb6c-c896c4048e48" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cross-layer consistency invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-b7ed-ee56d2b3db01" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{State}_{protocol} \xleftrightarrow{f} \mathrm{State}_{app}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-a324-eca82cd2dcb1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Application-layer reversibility must not alter protocol finality</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-bde8-e4a7fa502b98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi_{rev}^{app}&gt;0 \Rightarrow \pi_{rev}^{protocol}=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-911c-dbdedc879b8e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Institutional ledger reconciliation invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-9fa1-e0cc5a166e92" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Ledger}_{ext} = g(\mathrm{Ledger}_{proto})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-9bae-dd2da0038e49" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Policy commitment invariant across layers</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-a11b-c50af84bf869" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(Policy_{inst}) = H(Policy_{proto})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-9c54-ca693a530729" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fee exposure transparency invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-be72-dc8b693c33f5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{TotalCost}_{inst} = Fee + R_{comp}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-98fa-d9f3bcd3aaad" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cross-layer latency bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-9dba-c8b4827d1c33" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{e2e} \le T_{proto} + T_{app} + T_{inst} \le \bar T</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-9d17-f2c86ba51708" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Composability risk constraint</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-864c-cc6860b07bfa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \uparrow \text{ with } \#\text{composed modules}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-8995-fa9b7b232267" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Module isolation invariant</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-bc5c-eb94528ce241" class="">Failure in module  must not alter balances in .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-8a17-cab403912614" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Permission boundary invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-a948-d32adb971ecc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Role}_{app} \subseteq \mathrm{Role}_{proto}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-a413-f3454389f49d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>App-layer fraud must not escalate to protocol-layer loss</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-b22d-cc5cc445ce50" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL_{proto} \not\propto EL_{app}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-9572-f9963601bb5d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cross-layer observability condition</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-9c8e-e8d510ed1625" class="">All material state changes must emit protocol receipts.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-90b3-f04442d74d12" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Settlement–accounting equivalence condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-80a4-f07a0b4c2674" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Assets} - \mathrm{Liabilities} \text{ preserved across layers}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-ba7f-f415f712b080" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Reconciliation drift bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-9155-c49377817cbb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|\Delta(\mathrm{Ledger}_{ext} - \mathrm{Ledger}_{proto})\| \le \delta_r</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-a0dd-c0422468fe5f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Application diversity reduces correlated failure risk</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-902a-f9278d20933c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \downarrow \text{ as app heterogeneity } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-8b93-fd704e97cb5f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>But heterogeneity increases entropy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-99b0-fd03ba74cd7a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E} \uparrow \text{ as heterogeneity } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-970b-cd0b523be7d3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Optimal heterogeneity condition</strong><br/>Minimize:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-893d-c49a32effe44" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL + \lambda \mathcal{E}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-a5af-ede87cef3f08" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Composability audit requirement</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-ab51-d15c627059d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(\mathrm{DependencyGraph}) \in \mathrm{Log}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-86f5-daeb6806a20a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>External oracle dependence bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-9aea-e70e32468056" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \uparrow \text{ with oracle reliance } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-a3ce-fa1e1001a63c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Oracle diversification invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-8a3d-dc0d14c06628" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\#\text{independent oracles} \ge k_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-8cb2-cdf859fbb019" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cross-layer incident isolation requirement</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-a064-fc608edaa135" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Incident}_{app} \Rightarrow \mathrm{State}_{proto}\ \text{unchanged}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-9da7-ea67535f96de"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-aece-f04f27cbb653" class="">921–940: Capital Structure and Balance-Sheet Geometry</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-8206-f627818b849b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Capital efficiency ratio</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-8960-d793430bde26" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CER = \frac{Volume}{Capital\ Locked}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-8b3e-f89c30b8cc0d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>CER increases with lower finality time</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-b96b-cc697cb1cc10" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CER \propto \frac{1}{T_f}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-9d77-fc0361e312ac" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Haircut sensitivity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-abed-eed7496e586e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C \propto \frac{1}{Haircut}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-837b-d774616511f1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Haircut increases with volatility and MPL</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-9c56-f8e097e21035" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Haircut \propto \sigma_{value} + MPL</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-9654-c0e8e1be0e4e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liquidity buffer constraint</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-841d-f0414d46b474" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Liquidity_{buffer} \ge StressLoss_{est}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-8d90-cb43cd5e95bc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Solvency invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-9df0-d9c42d3b0ed7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Assets - Liabilities \ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-84b0-d0628ebdff23" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Leverage bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-89cd-f69d98e783f5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Leverage \le L_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-9363-fcf59087b46d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>System-wide leverage increases contagion risk</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-aa78-c475e6ab69a2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \propto Leverage^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-bb2a-eac53c332b0f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Capital concentration increases capture risk</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-beec-ee5d3ff62a2b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G \uparrow \text{ as capital concentration } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-b1fc-d2fc92e3b5ff" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Treasury diversification reduces correlated drawdown</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-9a32-e068fb6b9b7e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Var}(Loss) \downarrow \text{ with asset diversity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-ba58-f1ebed749114" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liquidity–confidence feedback</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-a6fd-c8089655fc77" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial L}{\partial N} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-a4d7-f76fd01d531a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Capital adequacy influences adoption</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-965f-ed95b3dda960" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial U}{\partial CER} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-8836-cafb2da2ff6d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Insurance reduces effective haircut</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-afcb-d4ca30aa9196" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Haircut_{eff} = Haircut - \beta I</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-a38b-ee5a9669e6fb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Collateral reuse increases systemic coupling</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-9b3c-ea95abbf711d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \uparrow \text{ with rehypothecation}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-9d5b-ea00142d4aed" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Rehypothecation cap invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-8658-ebd9d8e668fb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Reuse\ Ratio \le r_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-91c5-da981ffd4780" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Clearing fund adequacy condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-8f2b-fe0267e3918f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Fund \ge VaR_{stress}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-802e-d1956036c4d7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Settlement compression increases CER</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-8c7e-e3c357800bd7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CER \uparrow \text{ with netting gain}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-8772-cbebb261e65a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>But compression increases dependency on netting correctness</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-b225-f2030444e1d6" class="">Constraint:  mandatory.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-b18e-e393df5832a9" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Capital transparency reduces political risk</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-ae03-e09b5c4d4d58" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P \downarrow \text{ with solvency transparency}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-9446-ca5d5a6ce7d3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Balance-sheet opacity increases compliance cost</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-bf3c-df1375fb7d74" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{comp} \uparrow \text{ with opacity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-b3ea-d27b0dedeaa4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-9995-e85946988db3" class="">941–960: Migration Equilibria and Path-Dependence</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-bc12-efa115124db3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Migration cost function</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-8a0d-e2eae1b65716" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MC = S_f + Integration + Training + Legal</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-8451-cc647b36047d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Migration probability</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-8166-f4daeaec746f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(migrate) = \sigma(\Delta \Phi - MC)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-9397-f23c73ad8eca" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Incremental migration lowers threshold</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-bf43-fb61aac7e373" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MC \downarrow \text{ when additive compatibility exists}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-956a-c167ed71f83c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Coexistence equilibrium condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-9b75-e4ab658ba519" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_X &gt; U_{BTC},\quad \Phi_{BTC} \approx const</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-85fc-c251d6ba1dc6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Abrupt replacement increases systemic risk</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-b660-ee23b0f797a9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \uparrow \text{ during abrupt migration}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-9bf8-e8215300270e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Phased migration reduces MPL</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-bfaa-c155e56da37b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \downarrow \text{ with phase segmentation}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-aacb-dd43818095a5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Institutional inertia term</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-a894-ccdbcf9526be" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_{replace} \uparrow \text{ with LegacyLock}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-9524-d9e1d40c4cc9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Shock-induced migration</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-9665-c5d42de48cc3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta U \propto IncidentSeverity_{incumbent}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-a6e5-cb068128d66f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Category redefinition reduces MC</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-839b-d2dcad3809d8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MC \downarrow \text{ as evaluation axes shift}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-a1a4-eef59f59bde4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Switching cascade condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-abd6-c6ce3a6c95bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U &lt; U_{crit} \Rightarrow dU/dt &lt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-8b56-e992cbba4ec9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Hysteresis property</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-b237-d404ec171eef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_{replace} &gt; \Omega_{enter}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-a0cd-fea97a8815a4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Migration stability condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-92eb-fc16e171a6ae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall t \in [t_0, t_0+T],\ EL,MPL,P \text{ bounded}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-8f0d-de44d5c04742" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Training complexity increases entropy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-8170-c5126a93fc0b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E} \uparrow \text{ with retraining burden}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-bc76-f525fe2061c7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Standards compliance reduces retraining cost</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-8620-e7cb550f7f8c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MC \downarrow \text{ with standards alignment}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-b180-dcacf85ab566" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Migration must preserve legal enforceability</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-b5fe-ec4b25958d1f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Migration must preserve solvency invariant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-85c8-f63dad0b8ecf" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Migration must preserve audit trail continuity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-ab8b-c8c13812466d" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Migration must not alter monetary constitution</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-af65-f9430c6ab218" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Backward compatibility reduces shock amplitude</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-88f6-d8f8aee71cfa" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Successful migration increases embeddedness</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-9d8a-c0da92ad3291"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-9b5f-d00286e65a0f" class="">961–980: Behavioral Stability and Human Factors</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-beba-c9ed3aa2c3a9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Perceived fairness affects adoption elasticity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-8b53-e838baf080ca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\eta_U = \frac{\partial U}{\partial Fairness}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-8e5e-f662016f7fcb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Opacity increases rumor propagation rate</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-8e16-ea4826c99e1b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial P}{\partial Opacity} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-875e-e28d28925b60" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Rumor propagation model</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-b2e4-cc18a4d913e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dRumor}{dt} = \alpha Opacity - \beta Transparency</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-93e4-f6a2df4eb8af" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Incident communication delay increases rumor growth</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-86dd-e70edcd2821a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Clear incident disclosure reduces long-run MPL</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-bc6a-e079d605448e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Behavioral volatility amplifies price volatility</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-98e7-eb19a3ee3f65" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Price volatility increases haircut and reduces commerce</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-9522-d4061b2e882e" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Communication speed influences narrative recovery</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-bbbf-fd58e336a07e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dN}{dt} \propto -\Delta t_{disclosure}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-9379-f30763752a2e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Blame assignment uncertainty increases compliance cost</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-8898-d53312e3c513" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Clear accountability reduces political escalation</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-872c-fa0de99df7e4" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Human error probability bounded by UX quality</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-8092-ec3e3dfd75ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(error) \downarrow \text{ as UX quality }\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-9d57-c319b3a9929e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cognitive overload increases entropy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-94f0-f6a53578fcc8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E} \uparrow \text{ with overload}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-b80a-f07bba2ef196" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Training reduces error but saturates</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-82f3-f2185f8b9152" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Role clarity reduces internal fraud probability</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-8a77-c8c4448ddd09" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Overcentralized authority increases capture perception</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-92b2-d71dd759a02f" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Perceived capture increases political risk</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-bd6f-ed21de80b05c" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Public governance artifacts reduce capture perception</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-810d-cdc7a9e1e857" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Long-term stability requires trust compounding</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-8886-f3c1104dd9aa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dLeg}{dt} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-9c94-cf675acfb11f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Short-term opportunism reduces long-term Leg</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-a178-ddc65d9b9faa" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Dominance requires intergenerational trust persistence</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-b236-f08f3c50e788"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-aad4-c4954a513197" class="">981–1000: Terminal Constraints and Ultimate Closure</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-9456-c367f60fa9c2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Physical bandwidth bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-b95c-e6ee69cc35ba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Th \le B_{physical}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-9d36-f7ac8c933bab" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Energy consumption constraint</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-a221-db5f58201948" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Energy \le E_{sustainable}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-b323-f1e70989b407" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Human governance latency lower bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-aaf2-c193ad2a262b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_g \ge \tau_{human}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-9267-fe81b3be6446" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Zero-risk impossibility theorem</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-b97f-f8b7ef27e9ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL &gt; 0,\quad MPL &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-ad40-df86631f4b1c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Perfect decentralization impossible under coordination constraints</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-b034-eeb1643d0270" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Perfect centralization maximizes capture risk</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-adc8-fcae44471349" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Optimal governance lies in constrained middle</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-8272-e60c4a3dcdec" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Dominance persistence requires entropy minimization + capture resistance</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-bc42-e380858bc77a" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Entropy injection rate must be &lt; entropy dissipation rate</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-92cb-c52b89743b99" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Dissipation requires invariant enforcement</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-a8c9-eb7188aaa0a5" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Dominance metastability condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-9493-f969f4e1d4a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(J_F) &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-9d91-d3eb6e06541b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Global stress resilience condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-bb64-f9bb5fbb3bcf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1} \in \mathcal{D} \text{ for all } Z</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-8134-c870177c31a2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance is path-dependent</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-907c-c64927d0c5f7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Dominance decay requires multi-axis invariant violation</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-9582-d4792219f039" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Reserve asset and utility rail separation equilibrium possible</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-afd5-fa644ad4e815" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Utility dominance does not require reserve collapse</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-be8a-f7143e000f6e" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Collapse requires simultaneous spike in</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-a7e0-e359b5d6b412" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Best achievable system minimizes risk under physical and political limits</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-bd8a-fa7c4fe2269d" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Sustained dominance condition (extended):</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-80ed-e5176bf9405a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists T:\forall t\in[0,T],\ x_t \in \mathcal{D},\ \|\Delta x_t\|\le\delta_x,\ \rho(J_F)&lt;1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-b752-e5e2c4e90c0f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Grand Unified Dominance Principle:</strong><br/>A monetary-security system becomes dominant when it:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-9de6-cc0743fce173" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Minimizes entropy under bounded adaptability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-8745-e6e1c435f83f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Minimizes tail risk under bounded physical constraints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-8a52-eed241161827" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Maintains reflexive growth loops below instability threshold</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-829c-f262c97d5c10" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Enforces invariants across governance, liquidity, capital, and political domains</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-9abc-c907415a6717" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sustains these conditions over time horizon</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-82b9-f4adb18219f3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
U\uparrow,\ \Phi\uparrow,\ S_f\downarrow,\ EL,MPL,P\ \text{bounded},\ \mathcal{E}\ \text{minimized},\ \rho(J_F)&lt;1
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-9ae0-ca7f6a43e276"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-bf3c-c0aa7ec22974" class="">You now have <strong>1000 structured invariants, constraints, equations, and dominance levers</strong> spanning economics, security, governance, entropy, reflexivity, capital structure, control theory, and asymptotic limits.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-8bfc-dfd331d9b643" class="">At this scale, the only meaningful next steps are compression or formal proof synthesis.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-867c-e2db65e6cdbc" class="">Shared primitives (carry-over)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-a7c7-f85646177f6a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_f=\mathcal{E}+K+\mathrm{LegacyLock}+\mathrm{CoordinationCost},\quad
\mathcal{E}=\alpha K+\beta H+\gamma(1-F)+\delta\,\mathrm{Ambiguity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-aa54-f29205435f74" class="">Q=\frac{1}{T_f}(1-\pi_{rev})\frac{1}{1+\sigma_f},\quad<br/>\Phi=w_L\ln(1+L)+w_D\ln(1+D)+w_C\ln(1+C)+w_I\ln(1+I)-w_PP-w_GG-w_{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{E}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-9509-e22d26de7bc9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU}{dt}=\rho_1E+\rho_2Q+\rho_3F-\rho_4P-\rho_5S_f-\rho_6R_{comp}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-ac6c-c926a14aec20" class="">EL\le\epsilon,\ MPL(p)\le\Lambda,\ FeeSpike\le\bar f,\ Outage\le\bar o,\ Backlog\le\bar b<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-b8e4-cafd0c55381a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-96a2-fc649572528c" class="">701–800: 100 more (new control surfaces, invariants, and equilibrium levers)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-8cce-d04aca50aa38" class="">701–720: State estimation, observability, and control theory framing</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-8c11-e05354966bde" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>System must be fully observable at governance layer.</strong><br/>Define state vector (liquidity, fees, uptime, concentration, policy drift, incidents).<br/>Observability condition:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-886d-f06d9fd9d02e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{rank}(\mathcal{O})=|x|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-909c-fb93fc308655" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Unobservable state increases political risk.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-931b-cf3c437416b3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial P}{\partial (1-\mathrm{Observability})}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-804f-f0b47bccdfde" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Drift must be explicitly measured.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-8dff-d5e0dccd3aed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta x_t = x_{t+1}-x_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-9755-c412723fc201" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Drift magnitude must be bounded.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-8bf1-e8eb9a826896" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|\Delta x_t\|\le \delta_x</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-8239-e7278b2445b7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Control actions must have predictable impact.</strong><br/>For control input :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-97db-dac0948d14eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=f(x_t,u_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-9a8f-e8b60f9cbe0c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Unbounded control response increases entropy.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-8d3f-e8e651f999c8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}\uparrow \text{ as } \left\|\frac{\partial f}{\partial u}\right\|\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-aaec-d89d2493982e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Feedback loops must be stable.</strong><br/>Closed-loop eigenvalues:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-88c6-c77e3bb50c21" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\lambda_i|&lt;1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-a37a-c3812b26ce32" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Overreactive governance causes oscillation.</strong><br/>If gain too high:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-bb7e-f80d6ca48bdb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\lambda_i|&gt;1 \Rightarrow \text{instability}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-806f-d8ecb8b4cb15" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Underreactive governance causes drift accumulation.</strong><br/>If gain too low:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-8185-e8b8772002cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_t \|\Delta x_t\|\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-a8d2-db2576786efa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Optimal governance gain exists.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-866c-f3030efc9793" class="">Choose  s.t. stability and drift bound both hold.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-a5f9-cfd5e5c9b1df" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Incident frequency is a measurable state.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-94c8-da4950e95a4b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{IncRate}_t \le \bar i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-8809-e8559a1dc05c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Incident clustering increases MPL nonlinearly.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-85eb-ee4d6f658add" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \propto \mathrm{IncRate}^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-82a4-fe5af320bebd" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Variance of finality time is a control variable.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-8c91-d6d5165d2388" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma_f\le \bar\sigma_f</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-b2a1-e8578cfcc5d1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fee variance is a control variable.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-8c1f-e957b81c148e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Var}(Fee)\le \bar v_f</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-b578-e39942e1f64e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liquidity depth is an observable state.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-8314-eef2e4f1d705" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L_t \ge L_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-aec8-d49a411d1ec5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Derivative open interest is an observable state.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-966d-c46cb9a892da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_t \ge D_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-87f7-efb36c31ac57" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Concentration ratio is an observable state.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-8282-f7248788b4a9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c_t \le \bar c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-90aa-c9069a4bb925" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Policy drift is an observable state.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-8668-f2cae4e4ba53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|\Delta\theta_t\|\le \delta_\theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-ae74-f751e660c588" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Political risk proxy must be estimated.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-ad4f-eb612f26941d" class="">Let .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-a507-e148ba0dd2b6" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Control objective is multi-objective minimization.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-960c-dd5e3035463a" class="">Minimize:</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-bcaa-d8bd1b784d0a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
