J = a EL + b MPL + c P + d \mathcal{E} - e U</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-adee-daab50c61ebe"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-8bb5-c1412f4fe5ea" class="">721–740: Adversarial game theory and incentive compatibility</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-b1cb-e84a2e0ed081" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Attackers maximize expected payoff.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-a635-e94d31705d86" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{att}= \Pr(success)\cdot Gain - Cost</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-bc83-d44520a49566" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>System must ensure .</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-a32a-fada391513f2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(success)\cdot Gain \le Cost</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-9f7a-ccf7e6ba11e0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cost must scale superlinearly with attack size.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-bc28-f2a179b01527" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Cost \propto s^\alpha,\quad \alpha&gt;1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-adcb-fc8936aeab57" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Capture attacks must require coalition above threshold.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-b4b2-dacc46362485" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Min coalition size}\ge q_{capture}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-b3ed-c3ed30ffd9fa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Coalition probability decreases with diversity.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-8628-e204a22479c1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\text{coalition})\downarrow \text{ as diversity }\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-969c-f6e0fce14a63" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Sybil attacks increase with low admission cost.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-bf19-d5f90c5c9f06" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\text{Sybil})\uparrow \text{ as cost }\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-8824-ca73f2ac069c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Admission cost must exceed expected attack benefit.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-bd7e-fc7d327b4fa1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Cost_{admit} \ge \mathbb{E}[Gain_{Sybil}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-9542-d66eb73bb856" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Extraction attacks (MEV) are incentive failures.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-8d49-f9d43f38a251" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_{mev}&gt;0 \Rightarrow \Pi_{op} \text{ misaligned}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-b67d-f3ef029e8b46" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fair ordering must reduce extraction payoff.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-a3b2-efad704345d0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{mev}\downarrow \text{ with fair ordering enforcement}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-9451-ec6f0714a359" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Collusion probability increases with communication bandwidth.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-a991-fa9c7fe59d78" class="">Constraint: reduce coordination incentives.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-82c0-fb1026a75bd3" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Slashing must exceed collusion profit.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-8db6-f91fe5bb2d80" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Penalty \ge \mathbb{E}[CollusionGain]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-bdbc-cdafec6fb488" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Governance capture payoff must be bounded.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-81f3-e97d35fb13f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gain_{capture}\le \bar g</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-aaf3-cea14b333eb5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Emergency powers must not create capture incentive.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-a40b-c5e605905ab9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{capture} \text{ includes emergency leverage}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a5c6-d65eccd34899" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Time locks reduce flash attacks.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-b592-d135d2fbbc18" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\text{flash governance attack})\downarrow \text{ as } \tau_g\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-8c7c-ea166eff84c4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Information asymmetry creates arbitrage incentives.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-bcd8-f3e0270d4134" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{arb}\uparrow \text{ with info asymmetry}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-ad09-ca20761b78d7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Transparency reduces asymmetric advantage.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-9818-e8d0d794c3af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial \Pi_{arb}}{\partial \mathrm{Transparency}}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-87b6-c78fbf600613" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>But transparency must not leak strategic data.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-8ce2-c8d5a65e0f2b" class="">Constraint: .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-8544-db4b25c3b691" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Repeated-game incentives promote cooperation.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-9b0f-cd93c982da65" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{future}\text{ discounted by }\delta,\ \delta\approx 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9abe-eb6dce92a3a0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Short time horizons increase malicious behavior.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-9ff6-c4d6ae3b31b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(misbehavior)\uparrow \text{ as } \delta\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-b1cf-f80054029b66" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Therefore staking lockups increase horizon.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-aeff-e9b3a4f6235a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\delta\uparrow \text{ as lockup duration }\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-a651-c1fea6bee14b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-b69a-e441a80302d5" class="">741–760: Information theory and compression of governance complexity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-bd13-eb4e8fe3e55e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Governance description length must be bounded.</strong><br/>Let description length :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-807f-f22305d9a098" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
DL\le \bar{DL}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-b58a-e115027f28b6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Longer description increases cognitive entropy.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-978d-c3ea6557eb6d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}\uparrow \text{ as } DL\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-b6a9-d2025c35b2f7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Policy compressibility increases adoption.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-ac21-f6275329e19a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial U}{\partial (1/DL)}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-8f0d-d1bb2b230bf0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Invariant sets must be minimal and complete.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-8e63-d91f0e2c66ef" class="">Constraint: invariants cover all loss channels.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-a377-c437df421cbe" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Redundant invariants increase audit cost.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-9cfd-f89d1ce457fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{audit}\uparrow \text{ with redundant checks}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-becf-ca012ee4b395" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Missing invariants increase MPL.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-b274-d539028d6e4d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL\uparrow \text{ if invariant gap exists}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-8d02-e7418e3bb5e8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Optimal invariant set minimizes complexity for bounded risk.</strong><br/>Minimize:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-876f-d62c7ef473d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E} \text{ subject to } EL\le\epsilon,\ MPL\le\Lambda</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-8422-f590f4fc92a1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Information redundancy increases resilience.</strong><br/>But excessive redundancy increases cost:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-8213-d67741302e36" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL\downarrow \text{ with redundancy } \uparrow,\quad \mathcal{E}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-bdff-e91927015e90" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Optimal redundancy exists.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-9d7c-fb8b3c4d4ee3" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Protocol message size must be bounded.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-b3f0-fe09bf53c690" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|msg|\le \bar m</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-b07f-e7fe80f53507" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Large message sizes increase attack surface.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-9a93-f54b5fa64e14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(DDoS)\uparrow \text{ with } |msg|\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-8c4e-debf3941f55c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Compression reduces bandwidth attack surface.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-8223-f7e75536b0bb" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>But compression must preserve auditability.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-bc39-ef8039c017dd" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Entropy of policy state must be bounded.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-ac3b-f024865aec83" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(\theta)\le \bar H_\theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-9aee-e4608fc8b484" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Ambiguity in policy language increases entropy.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-9add-dee51ce75dae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}\uparrow \text{ with ambiguity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-9cde-d10537b1c364" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Formal language reduces ambiguity.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-ade3-e58a52db890e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Formal language increases initial cognitive load.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-ae5d-c35674a17418" class="">Trade-off:</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-a0a8-fad3e6458e0e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}_{short}\uparrow,\ \mathcal{E}_{long}\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-81ac-cfa9ce9a06db" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Long-term dominance favors lower long-run entropy.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-aea2-ce466081e9b0" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Information asymmetry reduces fairness.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-ba92-e156ed420625" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Fairness increases legitimacy .</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-84b1-edc5be823e4a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial Leg}{\partial Fairness}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-9a02-d91229c9edbc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-a583-e7b11dd14748" class="">761–780: Systemic risk propagation and network effects</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-9a3d-e5305cdaa37e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Contagion probability depends on interconnected exposure.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-9627-dc2200939b64" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(contagion)\propto \mathrm{ExposureDensity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-846b-f21d7928d5af" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Exposure density must be bounded.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-8bb5-d3b60ce0a83b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Interconnected leverage increases MPL nonlinearly.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-ab08-cc60831104af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL\propto (\mathrm{Leverage}\cdot \mathrm{Interconnect})^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-9494-fad442805746" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Leverage caps reduce MPL.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-9809-c0ca5b37283a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Transparent exposure mapping reduces contagion uncertainty.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-bce5-f169f3faaa5e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Hidden leverage increases political backlash.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-83b3-ec7c2c005b4e" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Stress test coverage must include cross-institution shocks.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-8f72-e785de3d2a10" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Settlement atomicity reduces cross-chain contagion.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-a7c0-f54b0e3e7dd7" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Liquidity buffers reduce shock propagation.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-b590-cf99afd7971e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta L_{shock}\downarrow \text{ as buffer }\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-94a6-ea893df7c8dc" class="numbered-list" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Public incident disclosure reduces rumor spread.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-a317-cf28b4799b5f" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Political risk increases compliance cost.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-9280-e89ba39c5655" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Compliance cost reduces adoption rate.</strong></li></ol></div><div friction.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-8ce9-e36bcb13f5e2" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Central clearing reduces ir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-88da-da5b26925100" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li><strong>Clearing default fund must exceed stress loss estimate.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-8ce6-c8ade668f955" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li><strong>Clearing failure must not rewrite ledger balances.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-9400-fd1a99bbbb8f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-b1ff-d1df979a65d5" class="">781–800: Long-horizon stability and dominance persistence</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-9ee0-e7c111a17364" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance requires time persistence of invariants.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-a8ff-e625c0f1fb9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists T: \forall t\in[0,T],\ \text{all constraints hold}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-aa6a-ef2ae5f8f5cc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Single major violation resets narrative state.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-932f-c68ce1eb73d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\frac{dN}{dt}\uparrow \text{ as recovery speed }\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-8445-fcf842e25a3c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Institutional memory creates inertia.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-8f77-dd44d7993b6e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\Omega_{replace}\uparrow \text{ with LegacyLock}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-96ab-dd0bb15357a4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance break requires exceeding replacement threshold.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-8f12-fef9913dd679" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Gradual utility migration is more stable than abrupt replacement.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-a10c-cb5c7c5cf643" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Utility rail and reserve asset can coexist in equilibrium.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-be66-e1d54566742d" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Stable coexistence condition:</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-b54f-c1fba349ae33" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U(X)&gt;U(BTC)\ \wedge\ \Phi(BTC)\approx const</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-a5c3-ead2f850ca55" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Collapse condition requires multi-axis failure.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-a70b-dd9ed7d2f15f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>If , , 
and spike simultaneously → regime shift.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-9821-d3e7ffc4a475" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Regime shifts are rare but nonlinear.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-ae83-ff97e6c42288" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Preparation reduces regime-shift vulnerability.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-abb6-fb1f0bb0eb14" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Adaptive governance must be bounded by constitution.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-8b55-f771ee456d8f" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Constitution without adaptability causes obsolescence.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9cd4-fd5e172daccf" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Adaptability without bounds causes capture.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-bcb9-d7613b829246" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>Therefore optimal zone is constrained adaptability.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-9397-d9e42cf5d699" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li><strong>Dominance is a metastable equilibrium.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-afb9-c8c7d12e4d4c" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li><strong>Metastability breaks when cumulative drift exceeds threshold.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-96c1-d3b6516e9fbe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_t \|\Delta x_t\| \ge \Theta_{shift}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-a933-dbb1f2743c94" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Final dominance theorem (extended):</strong><br/>A system becomes and remains dominant iff over sustained horizon :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-98a8-f00048fb83dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U\uparrow,\ \Phi\uparrow,\ S_f\downarrow,\ EL\le\epsilon,\ MPL\le\Lambda,\ P\le P_{max},\ \|\Delta x_t\|\le\delta_x,\ \forall t\in[0,T]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-a46d-e6cb5a4da0e8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-9500-c970209f7e5c" class="">You now have <strong>800 structural levers with invariants and equations</strong> forming a multi-layer dominance system across:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-952a-d9a31c6c95eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Governance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-980e-fa19e88c05ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Security</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-a449-c178dd6906ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Insurance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-9a0c-cb1a409b8fba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Liquidity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-806a-c0f6c7ba5228" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Derivatives</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-ad56-d27a180c2e9f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Political risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-b65c-c7764bdd5dbd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Control theory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-b5c6-e43611f5bee0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Game theory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-be40-d53d0a550840" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Information t
heory</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-a5e2-c598bb322888" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Systemic risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-9336-de384e1dae59" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Long-horizon equilibrium</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-a093-f7e105ea6969" class="">If you want to go further, the only remaining escalation is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-a8e9-f868711eb8c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Collapse everything into <strong>one unified dynamical system with explicit state vector and stability proof</strong>, or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-8d33-cad0e3d55773" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Convert this into a <strong>formal publishable theorem paper</strong>, or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-bd63-e0ba8ba1dde9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Compress 1–800 into <strong>10 irreducible dominance invariants</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-9fe8-e989e1161502" class="">Shared primitives (carry-over)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-a9e6-e48a2cba7838" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_f=\mathcal{E}+K+\mathrm{LegacyLock}+\mathrm{CoordinationCost}
\quad;\quad
\mathcal{E}=\alpha K+\beta H+\gamma(1-F)+\delta\,\mathrm{Ambiguity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-90d2-d3e51befac5d" class="">Q=\frac{1}{T_f}(1-\pi_{rev})\frac{1}{1+\sigma_f}<br/>\quad;\quad<br/>\Phi=w_L\ln(1+L)+w_D\ln(1+D)+w_C\ln(1+C)+w_I\ln(1+I)-w_PP-w_GG-w_{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{E}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-9ddc-efea5603194a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU}{dt}=\rho_1E+\rho_2Q+\rho_3F-\rho_4P-\rho_5S_f-\rho_6R_{comp}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-8b87-d6c340ada31e" class="">EL\le\epsilon,\ MPL(p)\le\Lambda,\ FeeSpike\le\bar f,\ Outage\le\bar o,\ Backlog\le\bar b<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-b7a4-d7beb20ee1a2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-90c1-fe725f245ab1" class="">501–600: 100 more (new levers; each tied to an invariant/equation)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-ba97-d020b0e7a3aa" class="">501–520: Settlement semantics, legal enforceability, and liability geometry</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-bc1f-ff39694128e5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liability clarity reduces political and compliance risk.</strong><br/>Let liability ambiguity :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-aa20-e4286ed366de" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{comp}=R_{comp,0}+k_LA_L,\quad \frac{\partial R_{comp}}{\partial A_L}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-89cd-f258fff86c20" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Legal finality must be time-bounded.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-95ca-cfb0ddc90b32" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{legal}\le T_{legal,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-a0df-d5f27da363a2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Authorization semantics must be richer than signatures.</strong><br/>Constraint: authorization is a predicate:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-a370-c44fca946033" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Auth(tx)=\mathbb{1}[\mathcal{P}(\text{roles,limits,approvals,time})=1]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-a61b-ef7963a8d94f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Policy-as-code reduces audit labor.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-8558-e42b54db8d44" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{audit}\downarrow \text{ as } \mathrm{PolicyFormalization}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-bf28-fad9f3d5f396" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dispute process must be layered, not ledger-rewriting.</strong><br/>Invariant:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-ae4c-f41f03b1eeae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}_{dispute}\cap \mathcal{A}_{rewrite}=\emptyset</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-b129-e94992a91107" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Error correction must be compensating transfers.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-b9cf-e74974d86291" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \text{balance} = 0 \text{ via } tx_{comp}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-9499-d3fa1cf6aa79" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Consumer protection requires bounded reversibility at the application layer.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-8050-f7db137b6d4d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi_{rev}^{protocol}\approx 0\ \wedge\ \pi_{rev}^{app}\le \bar\pi_{app}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-ae69-d6e8a9ca55b3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Merchant risk is a function of dispute rate.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-9014-e21e7e66a948" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL_{merchant}\propto \Pr(\text{dispute})\cdot \mathbb{E}[Loss\mid dispute]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-9405-c93928f18957" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Chargeback-like workflows can be insurance-financed.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-a0e2-db72b8370cc4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL \downarrow \text{ as } I\uparrow \text{ for dispute coverage}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-a354-db0405074a11" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Legal enforceability of operator misbehavior reduces tail risk.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-bc4d-e4cc7af644bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \downarrow \text{ with } \mathrm{Enforceability}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-9271-cf5fb8443fe8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Jurisdictional rule conflicts increase adoption friction.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-a2c0-dceb7e17a583" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K \uparrow \text{ with } \mathrm{RuleConflict}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-a077-e4e2c49e5331" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Standard contracts reduce rule conflict.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-9012-d3f342ed7c66" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial K}{\partial \mathrm{ContractCompleteness}}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-8b92-e2cb5de3b1ec" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>“Who is responsible?” must be answerable for every failure mode.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-8b56-f2fe88b9b207" class="">Constraint: responsibility map exists for each loss channel .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-a9fd-ff0c6202c16e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Indemnity architecture can replace protocol mutability.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-823c-f07b1ae11fbb" class="">Invariant:</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-b3c8-c540ba991c39" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{loss handling} \Rightarrow \text{indemnity fund or insurer},\ \neg \text{rewrite}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-bce3-f7aebc031c7e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Settlement receipts must be legally interpretable.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-857f-c767656f8a95" class="">Constraint: receipts include policy hash + version + signer set.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-a637-d48a879db138" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>System must support audit-grade evidence packages.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-8a74-ea7d82a1759c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{evidence}\le T_{evidence,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-aefd-f695d990bfee" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liability caps increase insurability.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-97df-d9cbd61a9f49" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \downarrow \text{ as liability cap } \Lambda_{cap}\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-bc31-d1e8da050d62" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liability caps must not undermine user trust.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-bc48-c1067c0f26b5" class="">Constraint: caps are disclosed and priced into coverage.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-8da2-f14cf0c65c6e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Dispute processes must be deterministic in timing.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-9de0-cdf06a448786" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Var}(\mathrm{DisputeTime})\le \bar v_d</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-8991-cc1efe5496c2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Legal clarity increases collateral acceptance.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-8e2c-e8e4dd2f1924" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial C}{\partial \mathrm{LegalClarity}}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-ba28-fff7aa33c095"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-8c8f-de4e9dce7132" class="">521–540: Treasury operations, batching, cutoffs, and accounting control loops</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-b58f-e1ef7b204324" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Batching is a first-class primitive for treasury.</strong><br/>Let batch size :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-95be-fcdfb60ca7cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{OpsCost}\downarrow \text{ as } B\uparrow \ \text{(bounded)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-94a1-d7f99be11213" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cutoff windows must be deterministic.</strong><br/>Invariant:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-b58b-cfb2391a8d7e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Tx \text{ after cutoff} \Rightarrow \text{next window processing}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-be8f-e5f4d7b6415c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dual control reduces insider loss.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-9cfa-caffd846a17c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(insider\ loss)\downarrow \text{ with quorum } q\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-b9a7-fa430101679f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Spend velocity limits bound blast radius.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-93f6-c0cc4f25417f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{MaxDrainPerWindow}\le \bar d</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-8961-dd201e099aec" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Treasury policy state must be committed.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-9f3d-e13660d96178" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\mathrm{SchemaVersion} \text{ pinned for } \ge T_{pin}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-a52f-f1d185c8fc50" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Transaction labeling reduces audit cost.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-b67f-ced3608bf62b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{audit}\downarrow \text{ as } \mathrm{MetadataQuality}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-9581-ef460187d7b8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>But metadata increases leakage unless bounded.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-9f91-fd592c332797" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J \uparrow \text{ with } \mathrm{Metadata}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-967e-f9dd9cbf0e89" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Treasury prefers predictable fees.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-8efc-c76e125c05c8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\frac{\partial U_{inst}}{\partial (1/\sigma_f)}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-917c-dac4d6559e7e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Corporate governance requires role hierarchies.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-bc94-cbe67e2c24e6" class="">Constraint: role graph  is explicit and auditable.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-9b20-dc208fcd67bb" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Intercompany transfers require entity separation.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-9cf5-f5924877d5fc" class="">Invariant: accounts mapped to legal entities.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-84dc-ffb0310ba631" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Netting reduces liquidity needs.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-b214-dc6b73c36b8c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L_{required} \downarrow \text{ as netting gain } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-b5cf-f574f59ce033" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Netting requires provable correctness.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-87f1-c99648edae3c" class="">Constraint: provide netting proof .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-b10a-d7ae398478b7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Treasury needs deterministic reporting calendars.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-8be7-ed89d9852dc4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{ReportTime}\ \mathrm{Var}\le \bar v_r</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-b46e-fa6e3ba7b1c5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Treasury incident playbooks reduce loss.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-ada0-d7d6e017487b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL_{ops}\downarrow \text{ as playbook readiness } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-959f-d92bb3c18f6f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Treasury-grade recovery must be time-bounded.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-9090-d42ed59c3775" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{recovery}\le T_{rec,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-bdad-f518c54429bf" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Treasury operations scale through ERP embedding.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-b81d-cdba79bb4132" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial E}{\partial \mathrm{ERPIntegrations}}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-aa1f-d7630aaa7299" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Treasury adoption increases settlement volume.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-9a36-e08ad49929e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
V \uparrow \text{ with treasury penetration } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-b1c1-eb624bd809bb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-93b9-cc92f161aab5" class="">541–560: Identity, fraud, and human adversary economics</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-abf7-e98ceeaeee0c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fraud is a measurable adoption limiter.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-8bf8-d8ba7f88d147" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL_{fraud}\uparrow \Rightarrow U\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-a810-eede515a5511" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fraud loss is bounded by authorization semantics.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-a520-de49468afe32" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL_{fraud}\downarrow \text{ as } AuthRichness\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-977d-c5a9d26bf17e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Recovery improves retail adoption but must not enable theft.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-959b-ed0068d5212c" class="">Constraint: recovery requires time lock + quorum.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-a973-ca995fb4b175" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Identity optionality is mode-based.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-ada1-e63a15fb5471" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m\in\{low,mid,high\},\quad m \text{ committed per account}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-9b63-f2632f033801" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Mode separation reduces political risk.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-84ab-cd4bb87979c0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P\downarrow \text{ as compliance-fit increases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-84d9-db13eb79a2fa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>KYC overhead raises coordination entropy.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-8d5f-e1387fec1cf9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}\uparrow \text{ with KYC friction}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-a4c3-cccce4b55fcc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Therefore KYC must be triggered by thresholds.</strong><br/>Constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-8e4f-ca758cd63b6a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{if } amount&gt;\Theta \Rightarrow m \leftarrow high</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-946b-d2fddf260f27" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fraud is reduced by device binding.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-9f2c-f82a25eb718e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(fraud)\downarrow \text{ as device attestation } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-a8e9-c0585c62cd48" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>But device binding raises exclusion risk.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-afe6-cbcfd76e179e" class="">Constraint: accessibility KPI must remain above threshold.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-8266-e437ed05df08" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Human factors dominate losses in practice.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-8463-f92cc3ea1e62" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL \approx EL_{human}+EL_{tech}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-8a6d-cc2ecb33b012" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Safety UX reduces fraud.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-9d6b-e9c903aa260f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial EL_{fraud}}{\partial UXQuality}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-a050-ea6b48d5f3bb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Education reduces fraud but has diminishing returns.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-825b-e2cee984b643" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL_{fraud}\downarrow \text{ with training, concave}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-a20e-fad79b63abef" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fraud incidents reduce narrative stability.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-b9fc-fb90977ddb4d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N \leftarrow N-\Delta N(\text{fraud incident})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-9b4a-ea6650beb369" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Narrative loss reduces liquidity.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-adcb-d85e71f4d3b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L \downarrow \text{ as } N\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-be9f-e309c08260d0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fraud insurance increases adoption.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-9a2a-efc6501a4cd5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U\uparrow \text{ with } I_{fraud}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-abe2-e6e6977c3621" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>But fraud insurance requires bounded MPL.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-a8df-d16d956257d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL_{fraud}(p)\le \Lambda_{fraud}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-84d6-fddc6b75e71a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fraud detection must be privacy-safe.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-be03-c4440fe1e9b2" class="">Constraint: detection uses minimal disclosure proofs.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-8cfd-fa8540081689" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>User remediation time affects adoption.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-a961-df7e3a11926a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U\uparrow \text{ as } T_{remed}\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-a53a-c1a55ffe6b57" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Retail dispute handling affects merchant acceptance.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-9fed-f1786701f9e9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{MerchantAdoption}\uparrow \text{ as dispute loss }\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-9c1a-ddfbb931a639" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Merchant adoption multiplies retail usage.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-8979-e804429c267e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
V_{retail}\propto \mathrm{MerchantAdoption}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-9348-c4e92ea24b9b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-9316-fdac832b160e" class="">561–580: Interoperability, migration, and cryptographic longevity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-b97c-c054e4ad8a9b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cryptographic migration readiness is mandatory.</strong><br/>Constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-91fa-c53268dab823" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\text{primitive break without migration})\le \epsilon_{crypto}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-8b6c-eb6cbe1f29d6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Migration must preserve auditability.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-8726-f13010e52cfd" class="">Invariant: old and new commitments are linkable.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-8c43-c8fe26511b63" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Backward compatibility reduces switching friction.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-bbd3-fd9acb243116" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_f\downarrow \text{ as compatibility } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-b55a-c6b1d5165e40" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Interoperability must avoid bridge tail risk.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-8a40-d47193615fd8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \uparrow \text{ with bridge exposure}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-8b5b-f4e63a5e93d2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Standard message formats reduce integration cost.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-93b2-e644252727c8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K\downarrow \text{ as standards compliance }\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-8927-c3b7ccc2fc3b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Canonical settlement representation reduces fragmentation.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-af50-dd331dd4dcc8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Fragmentation}\downarrow \Rightarrow L\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-8dc4-df57cb7325ca" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Asset wrapping must preserve finality guarantees.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-894a-e5078f86b35d" class="">Constraint: wrappers cannot weaken .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-b40f-c342615c212f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>On/off-ramp reliability is part of SLA.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-b98a-e9d85796b6a0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Outage_{ramp}\le \bar o_{ramp}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-8be3-db68a28eba20" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Interoperability requires deterministic mapping rules.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-9d18-dc032c11812c" class="">Invariant: mapping  is stable.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-8a0d-e6e7be28216b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Interoperability errors raise compliance overhead.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-b9ba-d39a2055c62b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{comp}\uparrow \text{ with mapping error rate}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-9316-ebaa9c05a1ce" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Version sprawl increases cognitive load.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9203-e925e2a0ec7a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H\uparrow \text{ with } \#\text{versions}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-a179-d7b55b2982e0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Version pinning reduces audit risk.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-9384-d3eed417f3de" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{audit}\downarrow \text{ as } \mathrm{VersionPinned}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-928d-eea2333ad60d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>But pinning must not block security updates.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-93da-c75c1add5641" class="">Constraint: emergency patch path exists under bounded procedure.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-97f9-e31597e4fd95" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Interoperability success increases embeddedness.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-9a98-dd5f367a47c3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E\uparrow \text{ with successful integrations}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-a0e6-c58da7a194fd" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Embeddedness increases liquidity and derivatives.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-bdc8-d397ebccad10" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial L}{\partial E}&gt;0,\quad \frac{\partial D}{\partial E}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-b3f8-f64e4976d8da" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Migration incidents cause narrative shocks.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-bc1d-fb909897ba60" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N \leftarrow N - \Delta N_{mig}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-95f4-ea36361c6a65" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Therefore migration must be rehearsed.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-9641-e470f45e102f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL_{gov}\downarrow \text{ with rehearsal coverage } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-83dc-dc2b25ffddd7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cross-network finality mismatch creates systemic risk.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-b892-f294621d686d" class="">Constraint: settlement horizon must be bounded across integrations.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-a454-dc7c8d748eea" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Atomicity is required for PvP/DvP across systems.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-816f-fb50529b62d8" class="">Constraint: atomic primitives or compensating controls.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-a3b3-eac68a599563" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Interoperability should be constrained, not universal.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-aafc-d1b1cf4f22a9" class="">Invariant: reduce complexity → , .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-a8c5-e7c6bcf498cf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-b03d-d495872db2f9" class="">581–600: Macro constraints, economic cycles, and dominance under stress</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-a974-ca4bb58774b4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance depends on behavior in risk-off regimes.</strong><br/>Let stress indicator . Require:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-8906-d9e82385c4dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FeeSpike,Outage,Backlog \text{ bounded under } Z=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-b956-c73a19feda49" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liquidity flight is path-dependent.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-9271-da4982b1f80b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L \leftarrow L - \Delta L(Z=1,\text{incident})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-95a7-da6f534a14be" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Risk-off adoption depends on perceived blame cost.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-95a6-f9f664de25b6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(adopt)\downarrow \text{ with blame cost}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-94aa-f400eaba0b1c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Blame cost decreases with certifications and standards.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-95a4-c642167c6bfb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{BlameCost}\downarrow \text{ as } \mathrm{Certified}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-a099-f86ab29053c0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Capital charges are the hidden dominance accelerator.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-9dd4-fd59f2ec34fc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CapLocked\approx \kappa E T_f</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-8f31-e58e675e7a03" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Capital charge reduction requires regulator comfort.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-aa52-dd39450c8d4a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\kappa=\kappa_0-\Delta\kappa(\Phi)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-885c-d1f788640d34" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Inflation regimes increase demand for non-sovereign rails.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-ac40-c1552bd890a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
V \uparrow \text{ with inflation pressure (contextual)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-b069-ed07aea16b1f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>But volatility discourages commerce even if it attracts speculation.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-95dd-e506f0cb7cd8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
V_{commerce}\propto \frac{1}{1+\sigma_{value}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-b333-c3c82042b87a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Reserve asset and utility rail can decouple under stress.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-8bdb-cc643ce6edce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U(X)\uparrow \text{ while } \Phi(BTC)\text{ stays high}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-b3fd-d178501b819c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Systemic contagion risk reduces collateral acceptance.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-a481-cd0bfd8edc47" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C \downarrow \text{ as contagion risk }\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-b35a-fa73daed66b6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Contagion risk is reduced by transparency + bounded leverage.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-a991-eea710e54c32" class="">Constraint: solvency attestations and leverage limits.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-956f-d559ff0200f3" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Leverage increases tail risk.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-afed-ec852ff3db13" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \uparrow \text{ with leverage } \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-b064-ce1574c51a9b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Therefore leverage policy must be explicit.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-8857-f4a8a633a9a3" class="">Constraint: leverage caps committed and auditable.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-acd0-e8e5a5d9ef95" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Stress tests must be published as artifacts.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-8626-ead8ca8f62ef" class="">Invariant: .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-8505-f726c46d7b3d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Stress performance feeds narrative stability.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-8f78-e3b1fca61ae8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\lambda(\text{capture})\downarrow \text{ as rotation frequency } f_{rot}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-8d67-f3896646d2ce" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Upgrade rehearsal reduces expected loss.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-9af0-cd64040f9ccb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
