\frac{\partial P}{\partial \mathrm{Opacity}}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-bbd6-e27bc0e5009e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Policy drift must be detectable as a delta series.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-89f1-c00ee780a88e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta Policy_t = Policy_{t+1}-Policy_t \ \text{committed and diffable}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-b5d9-eb0aed8da699" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Monetary constitution must include “no surprise” clause.</strong><br/>Constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-8a8e-eed053127363" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta^{money} \text{ changes require } \tau_m \gg \tau_g,\ q \gg q_G</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-b26a-eb4489aa6494" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Validator admission rules must be explicit.</strong><br/>Invariant:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-ae20-ffcae4d06337" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Join}(v)\Rightarrow \mathrm{MeetsCriteria}(v)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-82bd-e1a437d425d6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Validator removal rules must be explicit and reviewable.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-817e-d7c96ff5aaf5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Remove}(v)\Rightarrow H(\mathrm{evidence})\in \mathrm{Log}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-a0b6-eac84e472f23" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Governance must have bounded failure modes.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-aec2-c64da66a339d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\text{governance deadlock})\le \epsilon_{deadlock}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-b994-ebdb91b5e021" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Deadlock resolution cannot rewrite balances.</strong><br/>Invariant:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-9f16-d54fafc039aa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Resolution }\notin \mathcal{A}_{rewrite}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-9b70-d51c5401e10e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Constitution must define what cannot be governed.</strong><br/>Invariant:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-9ac2-e5f805c4958d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}_{forbidden}\neq \emptyset</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-ba2c-d5b4780f28ca" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Governance changes must be version-pinned.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-9268-e894522b71c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
v_{gov,t} \text{ committed in receipts}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-ab3e-fb271cd66759" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Operator economics must penalize downtime.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-b23d-f84e33d751f5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{op} = \Pi_0 - k_o \cdot \mathrm{Downtime}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-bfda-d6915783cd47" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Operator economics must penalize censorship.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-80bc-d8895febd10a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

E = \sum_c w_c E_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-96fc-d2f5085dd08c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Channel adoption follows S-curves.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-b75e-e63d02d6f2c4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dE_c}{dt}=r_c E_c(1-E_c)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-8941-db1dd3386594" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Anchor clients cause step changes.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-a40a-c19b49cd56c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E \leftarrow E + \Delta E_{anchor}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-96a8-c7c73b0355cc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Switching is easier when additive compatibility exists.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-b781-ebab5dc43f8f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_f \downarrow \text{ if supports incumbent assets/wrappers}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-9d42-e58bf57335cc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Distribution reduces cognitive load by defaulting decisions.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-8bac-ed1e1482eb4d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H \downarrow \text{ as defaults } Def\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-bb73-e4c8cede8f8e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Default wallet status dominates retail adoption.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-b08f-e7750a1e1b72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{retail}\uparrow \text{ if } \mathbf{1}[\text{default wallet}]=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-977a-d989c229f8a5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Procurement checklists act as hard gates.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-9252-c2076eaff3de" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\frac{\partial K}{\partial \mathrm{Bundle}}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-a1a9-e180919594fb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Compatibility with ISO-like messaging reduces .</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-80ab-cec6cf4c70ac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

E \leftarrow E - \Delta E_{shock},\quad N \leftarrow N - \Delta N</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-b4c9-e74c74534619" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>“Time-to-confidence” is a channel KPI.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-9caf-d65c77330f0b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{conf}=f(Q,F,I,A_{audit}),\quad \frac{\partial U}{\partial (1/T_{conf})}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-9bf4-e8d3ce931f0a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Distribution amplifies hysteresis (harder to displace).</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-a406-ee4dcbf60836" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

E &gt; E^\* \Rightarrow \frac{dU}{dt} \text{ accelerates}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-9e43-d3f041482dfb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Distribution must be crisis-resilient.</strong><br/>Constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-861a-ec941dfcb118" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Outage_{channel}\le \bar o_{channel}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-91e5-fe14e529bbad"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9c1c-cf90319037e0" class="">461–480: Monetary design constraints beyond “fixed supply” (credibility physics)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-8ac0-eadeff8819e5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Supply credibility is an amendment-barrier property.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-8507-e76014f7bc28" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\Delta\theta^{money}\neq 0) \downarrow \text{ as } (\tau_m,q_m)\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-9bfb-c81e7d61ed0e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Predictability beats immutability for institutions.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-b920-c805d6556bb9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Var}(\theta^{money}\ \text{process}) \downarrow \Rightarrow \Phi \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-ba40-f5a85dc91058" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fee policy must be bounded and declared.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-94d0-ec053e8ab62e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Fee \in [Fee_{min},Fee_{max}] \ \text{under crisis mode}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-9110-dfdac4cfb83f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Monetary system must define unit-of-account options.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-be4d-d663908d3755" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\frac{\partial C}{\partial A_{audit}}&gt;0,\quad \frac{\partial C}{\partial I}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-8ba5-c16e04f69a17" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Reserve behavior is a threshold on .</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-b8b6-f3937aa50bb9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\text{reserve})=\sigma(\Phi-\Phi_{min})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-9feb-f449650a9e3a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Monetary policy shocks reduce narrative stability.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-b4d5-d64da1c9cb70" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N \leftarrow N - \Delta N(\Delta\theta^{money})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-b181-cb8d9d26cb08" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Value volatility taxes commerce.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-adeb-fae0bf08d781" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
V_{commerce}\propto \frac{1}{1+\sigma_{value}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-b656-d5d861d39fee" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Capital efficiency depends on settlement horizon.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-ab4e-f8089d60c847" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Margin \approx \sigma \sqrt{T_f}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-bf3b-d9bfc45654b7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Capital efficiency improves derivatives liquidity.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-9db5-decb0d62c561" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D \uparrow \text{ as } Margin \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-bd08-e8bf5f32a9d4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Capital efficiency improves liquidity depth.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-be7d-de9e57f3ad7f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L \uparrow \text{ as } CapLocked \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-b194-fdd08b4d6cc9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Monetary legitimacy is multi-actor.</strong><br/>Define legitimacy with:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-bafc-d51f70bebdab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial Leg}{\partial A_{audit}}&gt;0,\ \frac{\partial Leg}{\partial I}&gt;0,\ \frac{\partial Leg}{\partial P}&lt;0,\ \frac{\partial Leg}{\partial G}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-b6a2-fe5c844f82a1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Legitimacy increases adoption and collateral use.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-a0ec-db09ada7dc1c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial U}{\partial Leg}&gt;0,\quad \frac{\partial C}{\partial Leg}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-9751-c687e0cd9d44" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Monetary transitions require cryptographic migration plans.</strong><br/>Constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-ad0f-f835125ed1d0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(\text{primitive break without migration})\le \epsilon_{crypto}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-ba9f-dd183bb34e16" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Over-flexibility increases capture risk.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-8062-fb4542dee299" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial G}{\partial m}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-a783-f7a820aafbb0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Over-rigidity increases operational risk in transitions.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-9a8b-d2e698a5cd81" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL_{ops} \uparrow \text{ if change capacity too low}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-aab7-fa25e5f89692" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Optimal is constrained flexibility.</strong><br/>Constraint set:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-b1be-e3d8d553f57c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta\theta\le\delta_\theta,\ \tau_g\ge\tau_{min},\ \text{but upgrades possible under strict procedure}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-bbd2-eea12931813f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Credibility is destroyed faster than built.</strong><br/>If incident:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-ad0c-c966e51c2280" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N \leftarrow N-\Delta N,\quad \Delta N \text{ large},\quad dN/dt \text{ slow upward}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-9c74-f98b7b140972"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-87ec-ca2cbffbb3d7" class="">481–500: Dominance-break stability, equilibria, and “no-free-lunch” constraints</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-9a9b-ffadb905183e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance is an equilibrium with basins of attraction.</strong><br/>Let dominance state be . Stable equilibrium when:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-82d8-d46178ca16e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dx}{dt}=0,\quad \text{and Jacobian eigenvalues have negative real part}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-bab1-c4101f2ec123" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Hysteresis: replacement threshold exceeds entry threshold.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-bfbc-f82af87f76bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_{replace}&gt;\Omega_{enter}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-9b76-d32c95aaf7b8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>The competitor must move 3 vectors at once.</strong><br/>Constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-9d68-fc99fce2942c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U\uparrow,\ \Phi\uparrow,\ S_f\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-b4f9-ee6c438b6672" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Partial wins don’t flip equilibrium.</strong><br/>If only but small:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-bbfe-f2fc1ac007a1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
dU/dt \approx \rho_2Q - \rho_5S_f &lt; 0 \ \text{(stall)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-be29-dfea8863e3a4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fragmentation raises MPL and lowers liquidity.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-81f1-d3f9be4859b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial MPL}{\partial \mathrm{Fragmentation}}&gt;0,\quad \frac{\partial L}{\partial \mathrm{Fragmentation}}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-8b99-c5cf4356fcea" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Bridge dependence is a tail-risk multiplier.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-942e-fa54ba92feb2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL = MPL_0 + k_b \cdot \mathrm{BridgeExposure}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-8cb6-cfcfde1fa96b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>If you can’t insure it, you can’t scale it.</strong><br/>Constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-b957-d13228588994" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I \approx \frac{1}{1+\eta_1EL+\eta_2MPL+\eta_3Ambiguity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-9720-ff8b88f5f321" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Political risk caps growth even with great tech.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-9c81-c0e750ff6286" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial dU/dt}{\partial P}&lt;0,\quad \frac{\partial dE/dt}{\partial P}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-a694-d2ee9558baac" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Compliance overhead is an effective fee.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-950c-fb516d715205" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Fee_{eff}=Fee+R_{comp}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-b90a-f9349e3452df" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fee predictability is more valuable than low fees.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-846f-c0e2d14e7713" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial U}{\partial F}&gt;0 \text{ often dominates }\frac{\partial U}{\partial (1/Fee)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-9eec-fe0abfc085dd" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Crisis performance determines long-run narrative.</strong><br/>If crisis invariant violated:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-97a0-f2ff1459b9fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N \leftarrow N - \Delta N_{crisis}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-9423-d42b4595b01c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Narrative stability affects liquidity via .</strong><br/>Let enter via reduced / Ambiguity:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-83cb-e9eb5b29d70e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi\uparrow \text{ as } Ambiguity\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-b839-dc9881c28bae" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Reserve and rail can decouple.</strong><br/>BTC can keep high while new rail wins :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-8b4f-dd5f88822a8a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U(X)&gt;U(BTC)\ \wedge\ \Phi(BTC)\approx \text{high}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-b5bb-c8154424cd3e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance break does not require BTC collapse.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-af7d-ff1583d5d2d9" class="">No equation needed; implied by 493.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-80f7-f66b5dcbb8b5" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>The “dominance break condition” is a conjunction.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-a2c3-fef89fa77396" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U(X)&gt;U(BTC)\wedge \Phi(X)\ge\Phi_{min}\wedge S_f(X)\le\Omega\wedge EL\le\epsilon\wedge MPL\le\Lambda\wedge P\le P_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-8b7b-fb6d150bdb1b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Stability requires keeping invariants true under .</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-9a17-e76fff793012" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FeeSpike,Outage,Backlog \text{ bounded in crisis}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-894a-f4487996ca50" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Every additional feature increases complexity cost unless bounded.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-9f15-ca277858bbe4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H \uparrow \text{ with feature count } n,\quad \Rightarrow \mathcal{E}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-a319-ea2c8f59ece1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Complexity increases governance capture surface.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-aa8b-e115e3467cfc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial G}{\partial \mathrm{Complexity}}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-b73b-d18705148766" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Therefore constrained design is a dominance requirement.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-94b4-ee4ce319f045" class="">Constraint: bounded primitives + bounded drift.</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-bcc4-cfb0f7652020" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Dominance is ultimately a coordination equilibrium under constraints.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-adee-c6a275f3ca63" class="">Formal closure: equilibrium flips only when (495) holds persistently over time:</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-bb9f-f40871796323" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists T:\ \forall t\in[t_0,t_0+T],\ \text{(495) holds}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-9b6c-ea59796bdda0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">--</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-bfc1-c428388a9a2a" class="">
</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-a972-e5f304da8a48" class="">Below is the “fill-in-the-missing” completion: a <strong>complete invariant + equation set</strong> that covers <strong>141–200</strong> point-by-point, without gaps. It extends the earlier envelope by adding the missing primitives: <strong>state, observables, update laws, constraints, and loop couplings</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-927d-d4e7524f4c84" class="">I. GLOBAL MODEL (ONE CANONICAL STATE)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-a8c8-e626f0063324" class="">I.1 State</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-9c9a-d3aeab0346a1" class="">For any money system  at time , define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-844d-f9b102b2ec7c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma_t(X)=\{L_t,D_t,C_t,U_t,E_t,K_t,H_t,F_t,Q_t,P_t,G_t,EL_t,MPL_t,I_t\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-8170-d20886040d3c" class="">Where (same meanings as before):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-a242-fedcc8d0111c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">liquidity depth, derivatives depth, collateral acceptance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-b852-e31a28ddc894" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">settlement-utility share, embeddedness/integrations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-9f20-ce051fac03c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">integration cost, cognitive load, fee predictability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-99d7-f853b2966118" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">finality quality, political risk, governance capture risk</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-a9da-d37072fd5760" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">expected loss, maximum probable loss, 
insurance availability/price index</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-a00d-df8fa3666e5d" class="">I.2 Observables (what you can measure)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-8e54-c67dfdc746aa" class="">Use measurable proxies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-9252-c682e34896d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">effective depth at impact</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-b3ac-d6272ed60af4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">normalized open interest + options volume + term coverage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-b900-d524a71775a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">number/quality of accepted counterparties + haircut quality</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-a80c-f7ac201be3f5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">weighted count of integrations (custody, ERP, payroll, PSP, 
CCP)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-a35c-d533056f1b7d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">time-to-integrate (days) normalized</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-83f5-eb3444b58c16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">internal approval steps + policy complexity index</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-a70f-d1073ab469c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-a597-c07c1e1ab975" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">defined below</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-9796-fa58e5843a0d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-81c3-fe07700e48ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">defined below</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-b32a-c8636b099506" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">from loss model + stress tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-8086-dd4d3a32c97c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">or capacity index</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9167-d7389b005e9f" class="">II. 
CORE EQUATIONS (THE MISSING ONES)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-93b5-e28a468c5e5f" class="">II.1 Finality Quality (covers 144, 196)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-a110-d73c0ffc16d3" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-ba3d-ffcadfaaa3df" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: time to finality</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-b62d-df85397a9167" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: probability of reversal after “finality”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-9941-c56b5c0fb770" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: variance of settlement time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-8c63-f52239c2390d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q=\frac{1}{T_f}\cdot(1-\pi_{rev})\cdot\frac{1}{1+\sigma_f}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-a384-c2649ea305c6" class="">II.2 Fee predictability (covers 166, 195)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-b03c-f369ab6878a0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F=\frac{1}{1+\mathrm{Var}(\mathrm{Fee})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-94ce-c2ea21635c02" class="">II.3 Coordination entropy (covers 141–143, 153, 200)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-83d6-ea08c1016f58" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-b5a3-ca408c28ebce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}=\alpha K+\beta H+\gamma(1-F)+\delta\,\mathrm{Ambiguity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-a13a-d36530c7cb82" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> adoption utility declines with entropy</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-89cf-d7c19c76adce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial U}{\partial \mathcal{E}}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-9f44-de5a721c575b" class="">II.4 Settlement utility share (covers 191, 200)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-8091-c3d646c54921" class="">Let value-flow throughput be . Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-89b9-f8e32bf78766" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U(X)=\frac{V(X)\,Q(X)\,F(X)}{\sum_i V(i)\,Q(i)\,F(i)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-bbe8-cca085f41da5" class="">II.5 Reserve/collateral “stickiness” functional (covers 152,156,157,192)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-bc15-c0fada3b1f77" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi=w_L\ln(1+L)+w_D\ln(1+D)+w_C\ln(1+C)+w_I\ln(1+I)-w_P P-w_G G-w_{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{E}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-bd6c-cf7f29e03bfd" class="">III. RISK, INSURANCE, AND “BOUNDED FAILURE”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-bf52-ef67a3b287f7" class="">III.1 Loss decomposition (covers 151, 158, 190, 193)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-8cfd-fe58739b8dcd" class="">Partition loss channels:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-afdc-c70f3d7bcf71" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL=\sum_{k\in\{ops,ins,ext,gov\}}\Pr(k)\cdot\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{Loss}\mid k]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-8ea2-f971bb232c98" class="">III.2 Maximum probable loss (covers 143, 151, 193)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-96e7-c2d4dd277fc7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL(p)=\inf\{m:\Pr(\mathrm{Loss}&gt;m)\le 1-p\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-bc62-d54f2e9786a7" class="">III.3 Insurability constraint (covers 151, 193)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-bba0-f04dc093b006" class="">System is “insurable at scale” if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-a411-e94bce1dac81" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL\le \epsilon \quad \wedge\quad MPL(p)\le \Lambda</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-bb02-f6bed5ec1cf7" class="">III.4 Insurance capacity / premium response (missing link)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-a6ec-e1af2317ce7a" class="">Let  increase with bounded risk:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-95af-c18aa97e2e08" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I = \frac{1}{1+\eta_1 EL+\eta_2 MPL+\eta_3 \mathrm{Ambiguity}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-a7e1-fb4e34f888ef" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong> better governance transparency reduces tail risk premium:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-8e10-da4c0f0db771" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial MPL}{\partial \mathrm{GovTransparency}}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-a4f4-fbdb576a0cc9" class="">IV. GOVERNANCE CAPTURE, DRIFT, EMERGENCY BOUNDS</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-914a-c1420524a351" class="">IV.1 Capture hazard (covers 169, 181–189)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-ac79-d706d7d02723" class="">Let concentration , amendment power , timelock .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-865b-f954f3a96f09" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G \equiv 1-\exp(-\lambda(c,m,\tau_g)\Delta t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-8b86-f0ba4d16ed2e" class="">with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-acb0-d9c4dd961348" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial \lambda}{\partial c}&gt;0,\quad
\frac{\partial \lambda}{\partial m}&gt;0,\quad
\frac{\partial \lambda}{\partial \tau_g}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-9329-f4bf0098843e" class="">IV.2 Drift per governance act (covers 169, 187)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-8fb4-d8ad50280c9b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta\theta = \lVert \theta_{t+1}-\theta_t\rVert</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-ae31-f79b369c9654" class=""><strong>Invariant (bounded drift):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-85fc-dd606c7d78fb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta\theta \le \delta_\theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-b92d-d38a19b3ffe8" class="">IV.3 Monetary constitution lock (covers 159, 187)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-b00a-e73d06dc3864" class="">Let  be monetary parameters (issuance, supply cap, redemption rules).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-829e-e722f1867932" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta^{money}_{t+1}=\theta^{money}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-88f0-d98a6de15379" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{SuperMajority}\wedge \mathrm{MultiJuris}\wedge \mathrm{TimeLock}(\tau_m)\wedge \mathrm{PublicArtifact}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-8536-decde2b5b27d" class="">IV.4 Emergency powers bounded (covers 184, 190)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-aadd-f44ec16b4d9d" class="">Define action sets:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-9795-d13979257c8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: ledger rewrite actions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-9ec9-f810aa0abb45" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: emergency actions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-b458-c4a39d3a5ecd" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-b1cd-d9d46bff9a2a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}_{em}\cap \mathcal{A}_{rewrite}=\emptyset</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-b9c0-d8b03936330e" class="">V. POLITICAL RISK AND “LEAST-THREATENING” DYNAMICS</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-9831-e644c501d9c6" class="">V.1 Political risk aggregation (covers 148–150, 170, 183)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-853b-fe17767fb354" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P=\sum_{j\in J} p_j \cdot \mathrm{Impact}_j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-9b86-d14d071c6adb" class="">V.2 Threat posture mapping (missing link)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-9028-d1c3bdcf2f80" class="">Let “perceived threat” be  (a function of narrative, surveillance, sanction posture, sovereignty clash). Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-944c-f50003a762f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_j = \sigma(a_j + b_j T)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-8e31-f181263ff752" class=""><strong>Invariant (149):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-b7c4-f7e0680a034b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial P}{\partial T}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-848d-ea14618329a0" class="">VI. EMBEDDING, DISTRIBUTION, AND INERTIA</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-a702-f7d7862f64ac" class="">VI.1 Embeddedness growth (covers 161–166, 199)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-83f8-e2ab78f325df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dE}{dt}=\eta_1 \mathrm{Tooling}-\eta_2 K</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-b74f-d4ae4aa1a33e" class=""><strong>Invariant:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-a4e4-db41d4c077fb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial U}{\partial E}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-af5f-d7b4db72af48" class="">VI.2 Integration cost decay with references (missing link)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-97fd-d724902ed292" class="">Let reference implementations and vendor bundles reduce integration cost:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-8193-c7d8ce2c14bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dK}{dt}=-\kappa_1 E -\kappa_2 \mathrm{ReferenceStacks}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-ac92-ec5d0257bf4b" class="">VI.3 Inertia term (covers 153, 163, 199)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-9cc5-ca8c021de5e8" class="">Let switching friction be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-a498-ed4018dff605" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_f = \mathcal{E}+K+\mathrm{LegacyLock}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-8dde-c4e263d7a168" class="">Adoption acceleration is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-873d-ca49d266d229" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU}{dt}=\rho_1 E+\rho_2 Q+\rho_3 F-\rho_4 P-\rho_5 S_f</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-b465-d7dcd1bfb5d1" class="">VII. LIQUIDITY–DERIVATIVES–COLLATERAL FLYWHEEL</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-a0c5-eb6b1a24a436" class="">VII.1 Loop dynamics (covers 152, 156, 192)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9807-d30ee41021f4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dL}{dt}=a_1 U + a_2 \Phi - a_3 P - a_4 S_f</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-a482-de460d9fc373" class="">\frac{dD}{dt}=b_1 L - b_2 \mathrm{ModelRisk}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-811e-d92e9c9f4ea0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dC}{dt}=c_1 L + c_2 D + c_3 I - c_4 P</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-a13d-f48c832f4d98" class=""><strong>Invariant flywheel:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-be4d-f8bb1a543b98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L\uparrow \Rightarrow D\uparrow \Rightarrow C\uparrow \Rightarrow \Phi\uparrow \Rightarrow L\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-815c-fde125687160" class="">VIII. CRISIS PERFORMANCE AND “FAILURE PREDICTABILITY”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-b025-d883f89d1cfb" class="">VIII.1 Crisis regime indicator (covers 147, 189–190)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-8322-defa77fe4bfa" class="">Let  indicate crisis.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-b7b2-fd0fd67e650b" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-aebb-edc75d34011b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-8a24-cb82e2edb192" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-831b-d5c3f5f37377" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-b6d0-d216751ec134" class=""><strong>Crisis invariants (143, 189, 190):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-b0e7-cc0335b053a6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FeeSpike \le \bar f,\quad Outage \le \bar o,\quad Backlog \le \bar b</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-8030-c1821bd741eb" class="">VIII.2 “Failure predictability” metric (missing link)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-a648-c0334338fb63" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FP=\frac{1}{1+\mathrm{Var}(\mathrm{RecoveryTime})+\mathrm{Var}(\mathrm{Fee}\mid Z=1)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-999a-c48f7ee51b30" class="">Institutions prefer higher .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-85db-fbebc473b534" class="">IX. THE DOMINANCE BREAK CONDITION (FINAL, COMPLETE)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-ad74-e4c04bfa9269" class="">A system  breaks Bitcoin dominance structurally when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-9836-fa13e1523c2e" class="">(A) Utility dominance</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-928a-e1c50a05c202" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U(X) &gt; U(BTC)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-ab20-ee828bce2a3e" class="">(B) Credibility threshold (collateral/reserve eligibility)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-a81f-d8c7d4e28916" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi(X)\ge \Phi_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-8368-c9896efd9f3e" class="">(C) Insurability at scale</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-9be6-c65e19124f32" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL(X)\le \epsilon \;\wedge\; MPL_X(p)\le \Lambda</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-a5db-ffdade82d0ab" class="">(D) Political survivability threshold</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-a7c4-c7e0b7c627c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(X)\le P_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-ac3d-fba69e3b5946" class="">(E) Switching friction below adoption threshold</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-8923-cda15599a43a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_f(X)\le \Omega</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-9946-f0a51d9fd905" class="">That is the complete condition set consistent with 141–200.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-8d34-d6d0a467072a" class="">X. 
“ALL MISSING” INVARIANTS CHECKLIST (ONE LINE EACH)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-8401-e93631a0a9f8" class="">These are the invariants you can publish as constitutional constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-a420-c8bbaa9c3d24" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Undetected rewrite infeasible:</strong> public commitments prevent stealth history edits.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-bf4a-dd1b15236b83" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Deterministic finality bound:</strong> , .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-8ed1-f4b2e3a87a08" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Stable under stress:</strong> , , .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-955d-d628ea2cf959" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Bounded governance drift:</strong> .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-a659-d7673429b88d" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Monetary rule lock:</strong> immutable except extreme procedure.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-8737-cd4cefebe806" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Emergency cannot rewrite ledger:</strong> .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-ba7f-eee3bd7fe854" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Anti-capture:</strong> and enforce caps to keep low.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-b304-ef6d6053675e" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>Insurable:</strong> , 
.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-a8a1-e5c402aafc5a" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li><strong>Distribution compounding:</strong> .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-b77d-e35eda120b6f" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li><strong>Entropy penalty:</strong> .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-9549-ea4fe17a62e1" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li><strong>Flywheel:</strong> .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-8678-c17bd375662c" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="12"><li><strong>Political threat penalty:</strong> .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-80ac-d27ad7dd378b" class="">If you want the next “completion” step, I can also provide the <strong>explicit mapping table</strong> from each point <strong>141–200</strong> to exactly which equations/invariants above justify it (one-to-many mapping), but the set above already includes all missing formal pieces needed to support the full 100.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-a403-ff43d383582f" class="">Below is a <strong>mathematical envelope</strong> (state variables, equations, invariants) that formalizes items <strong>141–200</strong> as a coherent system. 
It is not “one true model”; 
it is a <strong>deterministic scoring/constraint framework</strong> that lets you make rigorous claims and compare systems.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-8c74-ea14a05b9efa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-bc42-f6cdaa20370c" class="">0) State Variables</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-a608-da73024e5520" class="">Let system  at time  have state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-9b26-e6072018db3a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Adoption/usage</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-b6f5-ccb1735ed0af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: settlement-utility share (normalized )</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-b966-e37701ff68f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: active institutional participants (count or index)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-8de7-fd19bd1d2a4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: transaction volume (value/day)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-87ce-c24c4770ae01" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Liquidity/markets</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-8932-cc71539ed96d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: liquidity depth (e.g., effective depth at price impact)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-b614-fe38b9e4b9e9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: derivatives depth (open interest, options volume, 
term structure completeness)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-b6f7-f219c8faa900" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: collateral acceptance index (how widely and cheaply accepted)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-b928-d7486029f4b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Governance/operations</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-bcc2-e715ac9575e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: governance capture probability (per unit time)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a003-ebcd9b3a1f2d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: parameter drift magnitude per change</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-8b8c-d39f7a4b88e9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: governance change timelock (time)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-99c5-ed06fc5c5d64" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: operational uptime/latency guarantees (index)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-bc0b-eab5a7c46885" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Risk/insurability</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-ad51-f3d489a8bb93" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: expected loss (per year) for a representative institution</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-bb49-d78f63b43fff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: maximum probable loss at confidence (e.g., 
99.9%)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-86ac-fdeda30bd6b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: insurance availability/price index</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-954b-c719b67eef71" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Cost/friction</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-87cc-deff02637dca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: fee predictability (inverse volatility of fees)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-a336-d1d6f22ae4b3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: integration cost (time/money to integrate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-a57d-c0e83c1bee22" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: cognitive load (legibility burden for stakeholders)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-80da-ee05c0c39ef1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Political survivability</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-89a2-ff48658f27e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">: political risk (probability-weighted suppression/constraint events)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-85a4-f547f15b2fbc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-bbad-dbdad2be8e0f" class="">1) Core Objective Functions</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-bc07-fb1ddfccc076" class="">1.1 Dominance share</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-8e20-ea1d45f762fc" class="">Let market dominance (by value) be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-87fe-c3787618144d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Dom_t(X) = \frac{M_t(X)}{\sum_i M_t(i)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-829a-fcdaf09432e2" class="">1.2 Reserve-asset “stickiness” functional</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-86bd-e6037dad771c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Phi_t(X) = w_L \ln(1+L_t) + w_D \ln(1+D_t) + w_C \ln(1+C_t) + w_S S_t - w_P P_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-8bac-f711716b6bab" class="">1.3 Settlement utility share (rail dominance)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-9a06-c50eb856a8e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t(X)=\frac{V_t(X)\cdot Q_t(X)\cdot F_t(X)}{\sum_i V_t(i)\cdot Q_t(i)\cdot F_t(i)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-a620-d8172e1ebcca"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-9ca9-f2814b642771" class="">2) Finality Quality and Capital Efficiency</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-a38c-e62b16e7663a" class="">2.1 Finality quality</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-b86f-e739fb976780" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-b828-c4a1eca4e0fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: time to finality</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-a665-efe531337581" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: probability of reversal after “finality”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-8bd6-d96dbd4eb310" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: variance of settlement time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-8d30-eccbac2637b9" class="">One workable metric:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-b4c1-d86cbb1cd1e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q = \frac{1}{T_f}\cdot (1-\pi_{rev})\cdot \frac{1}{1+\sigma_f}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-a3fd-cf1a492eb25f" class="">2.2 Capital locked by settlement</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-b0ee-f13ddbb494ef" class="">For an institution with exposure flow  and required capital factor :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-8dca-d558b421271b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CapLocked \approx \kappa \cdot E \cdot T_f</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-940f-f507f71e54b3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_f \downarrow \;\Rightarrow\; CapLocked \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-a854-f38ca6bb9b5c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-94d6-f4a4c404844d" class="">3) Entropy / Friction Model (141–143, 149, 153, 199–200)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-ab14-c988e151206f" class="">Define <strong>coordination entropy</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-996a-d2b872d895d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}_t = \alpha K_t + \beta H_t + \gamma Var(Fee_t) + \delta \, Ambiguity(Gov_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-8e5b-dcd629dd9b10" class=""><strong>Invariant (141,142):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-a08c-c5a0f0fd0443" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU_t}{d\mathcal{E}_t} &lt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-b637-f07f43bf7b99"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-af20-e83063480c02" class="">4) Insurability and Loss Bounding (151, 153, 158, 193)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-bd85-e8a2a4c26954" class="">4.1 Expected loss decomposition</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-b07b-e88df78818bc" class="">Let loss channels be mutually exclusive:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-af22-fe4d91620962" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: operational failure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-a850-c2dcfeffceed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: insider abuse</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-9395-c25c5a5da0d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: external compromise</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-a101-ed0681eaeb39" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: governance abuse/upgrade failure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-981b-fb12b177dcd1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL = \sum_{k\in\{ops,ins,ext,gov\}} \Pr(k)\cdot \mathbb{E}[Loss\mid k]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-9b8b-cb85460fad9a" class="">4.2 Maximum probable loss</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-90f6-cb67e4a0804e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL(p)=\inf\{m:\Pr(Loss&gt;m)\le 1-p\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-89b8-e0557836f97f" class=""><strong>Insurability constraint (151,193):</strong><br/>Insurance becomes cheap and available when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-b16e-cf4090b6aeac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL(p)\le \Lambda \quad \text{and}\quad EL\le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-b7ed-fa3d01212653" class=""><strong>Invariant (158):</strong> transparency + constrained governance reduces tail risk premium:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-94b4-fa421f47407f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dMPL}{d(\text{GovTransparency})}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-909a-fc9274656661"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-8fa0-c8a3d5c027bc" class="">5) Governance Capture, Drift, and “Constrained Flexibility” (159, 169, 181–189)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-a5e2-ed0adec585de" class="">5.1 Capture probability as a function of concentration and control</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-8e59-fc4233e8740a" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-8724-e2062994ad52" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: concentration (validator/pool share or governance key concentration)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-8c79-fff727a6890e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: amendment power (how much can change per governance action)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-99b5-ca5cdb36637d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: timelock</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-84b2-c6af6fbee0fa" class="">A simple hazard model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-98bb-ccd6a33b0998" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_t \equiv \Pr(\text{capture in }[t,t+\Delta t]) \approx 1-\exp(-\lambda(c,m)\Delta t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-9568-e759e68b7a36" class="">5.2 Drift per upgrade</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-b694-fbf2e6a19042" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta\theta_t = \lVert \theta_{t+1}-\theta_t\rVert</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-a488-c4544a1b2a2c" class=""><strong>Governance invariants (169,187):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-a6a1-cc0a965eb112" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded change per step</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-bb97-de0c8103e712" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta\theta_t \le \delta_\theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-930c-d98696e022be" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta^{money}_{t+1}=\theta^{money}_t \quad \text{unless supermajority + timelock satisfied}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-9f0f-ebf8e76b7e9d" class="">5.3 Emergency powers boundedness (184,189–190)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-b255-cac1d3406f98" class="">Let emergency action set be . Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-94f5-d92de7a8bc50" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}_{em}\subset \mathcal{A} \quad\text{and}\quad \forall a\in\mathcal{A}_{em}: \; a \notin \mathcal{A}_{rewrite}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-98fe-e1b4ba8063fe"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-8faa-c2bc64234745" class="">6) Liquidity, Derivatives, Collateral Flywheel (152,156,192)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-b157-cb195a9581f3" class="">6.1 Collateral acceptance as a function of liquidity and legal clarity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-8a29-fa033ea7c2e6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t = f(L_t, D_t, \text{CustodyQuality}_t, \text{LegalClarity}_t, I_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-a152-fc44bbf7665d" class="">6.2 Reserve stickiness feedback</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-954d-ccfc0b954148" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dL_t}{dt} = a_1 \Phi_t - a_2 P_t - a_3 \mathcal{E}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-80ad-c1625c08811a" class="">\frac{dD_t}{dt} = b_1 L_t - b_2 \text{ModelRisk}_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-8eb6-c2719c85fb7a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dC_t}{dt} = c_1 L_t + c_2 D_t + c_3 I_t - c_4 P_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-9d68-e65b4b0dc939" class=""><strong>Flywheel invariant (152,192,193):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-896c-e7573796df53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L\uparrow \Rightarrow D\uparrow \Rightarrow C\uparrow \Rightarrow L\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-a6b2-e86fcf5722e0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-ad48-c628fa0cdb0d" class="">7) Political Survivability (148–150, 155, 170, 183)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-8a42-ee86939a5a7a" class="">Let political risk be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-9fcf-ca752297a883" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t = \sum_{j\in J} \Pr(\text{constraint event in }j)\cdot Impact_j</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-94c3-fb483964052f" class="">Hybrid systems can reduce  by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-9145-d4ea7d3581ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">jurisdictional distribution of control keys</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-8ddd-d0946e591ef2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">neutrality posture</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-a1fa-f873b6486e59" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">compliance optionality (mode separation)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-b117-cbfb20a0925d" class=""><strong>Invariant (149,183):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-b425-cea26e82f39a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{PerceivedThreat}\downarrow \Rightarrow P_t\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-a2bf-ccffdfb1a581"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-bab4-d4ad774089d9" class="">8) Infrastructure Embedding and Distribution (161–166, 197–200)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-bb9b-fa1f190237da" class="">Let  be “embeddedness” (number/strength of integrations: custody, ERP, payroll, payment processors).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-869e-c6482aac67af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dE_t}{dt} = \eta_1(\text{IntegrationTooling}) - \eta_2 K_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-96ac-db0c012a6175" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU_t}{dt} = \rho_1 E_t + \rho_2 Q_t + \rho_3 F_t - \rho_4 \mathcal{E}_t - \rho_5 P_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-8f67-fcfc95cc8e45" class=""><strong>Distribution invariant (199):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-839b-eff633137fdd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU_t}{dE_t} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-b4ae-f16c09ddb857"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-b73c-cc4a6448c907" class="">9) Crisis Performance (147, 189–190)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-980f-c851a54c4b68" class="">Define stress regime indicator  (normal vs crisis).
