\Pr(\mathrm{deadlock})\le \epsilon_{deadlock}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-ae78-f6c0c8700653" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Governance deadlock probability must be bounded.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-ba2a-db44941e39aa" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Operator replacement mechanisms reduce clique formation.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-8115-e32cf0686b41" class="">Constraint: rotation frequency .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-8129-d54772b790ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_{mev}\downarrow \text{ as fair-ordering score }\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-889e-f71b26bd219f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Rewarding fair ordering reduces extraction.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-8a5c-dec52f3e9047" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL_{mev}\propto X_{mev},\quad X_{mev}\le \bar X</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-8b17-ff63fbc5b59d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Economic incentives must discourage MEV/extraction.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-9b81-e096e1c91fef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(\mathrm{emergency\ action})\in \mathrm{Log}\ \wedge\ t_{action}\ \text{included}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-a024-c36df9fcc0f3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Emergency operations must be publicly time-stamped.</strong><br/>Invariant:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-9ab6-facbcdb9823d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{em}\ge q_{min},\quad \max_j \mathrm{KeyShare}^{em}_j\le \chi_{em}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-8a93-fc402808327e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Emergency keys must be thresholded and geographically split.</strong><br/>Constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-9dcb-c67093da7871" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta^{money}\ \text{cannot be altered by simple token majority}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-9cc8-f8ac70f58443" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Constitutional rules must dominate token politics.</strong><br/>Invariant:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-8cc6-cbddef0f8575" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Cost}_{abuse}\ge C_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-9a96-cafa2a0713ed" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Governance must be economically expensive to abuse.</strong><br/>Constraint: attackers must buy costly resources to change rules:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-bc1c-e259509406d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}\uparrow \text{ with } n_\Delta\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-8793-c158c598b5e9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Governance incentives must penalize unnecessary change.</strong><br/>Let change count :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-ac1d-c539b3d33952" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{fee}\le \bar D_{fee}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-a612-d7b0b37950bc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fee manipulation must be measurable.</strong><br/>Define fee deviation :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-9d17-ccb315e880fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{incl}\le \bar D_{incl}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-bb1f-e51710ddca1a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Soft censorship must be measurable.</strong><br/>Define inclusion disparity :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-907e-dc32abcd3bf3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Slash}(v)\Rightarrow H(\mathrm{evidence})\in \mathrm{Log}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8064-9443-ee80ab22e816" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Slashing must be tied to provable misbehavior.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-81c3-df4855ef2a34" class="">Invariant:</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-903b-ffdd28600740" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Validator diversity is engineered, not assumed.</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-a702-cea99edc88ec" class="">Constraint: admission requires diversity contribution score .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-8c68-f4b3dfff4fd3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL \uparrow \text{ with } \rho_{fail}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-89d3-f337c3573643" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Economic incentives must penalize correlated failures.</strong><br/>Let correlation :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-bf76-d9e74511c209" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\max_j \mathrm{ControlShare}_j \le \chi</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-9bfc-ea8e413d23e3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Jurisdictional concentration is a separate capture axis.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-8196-ebe4a7bf00a1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c \le \bar c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-b7d8-e64b721710d2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Therefore concentration must be capped by rule.</strong><br/>Constraint:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-99c1-f1033886fbd9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial G}{\partial c}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-929f-e24bbff325d3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Stake concentration increases capture probability.</strong><br/>Let concentration :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-9fb1-e46100a8d401" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial EL_{ops}}{\partial \Pi_{op}}&lt;0 \text{ via uptime term}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-b5f3-cc3039a45e0c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Reward schedules that favor uptime reduce expected loss.</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-a5c1-e31911d87d7d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{op}=\Pi_0 - k_o\cdot \mathrm{Downtime}-k_f\cdot \mathrm{FeeInstability}-k_c\cdot \mathrm{CensorshipEvidence}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-8722-cd9d497c57af" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Operator incentives must maximize long-horizon reliability, not short-horizon profit.</strong><br/>Let operator utility :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-a6cf-d73a1a40e7ec" class="">601–620: Governance–economics coupling and capture-resistant incentives</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-914d-fa1fe8d91e42" class="">601–700: 100 more (new levers, same formal style)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-ae90-f42593429845"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-a03e-cc2d90d18cd1" class="">EL\le\epsilon,\ MPL(p)\le\Lambda,\ FeeSpike\le\bar f,\ Outage\le\bar o,\ Backlog\le\bar b<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-97e5-ea2539ff519f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU}{dt}=\rho_1E+\rho_2Q+\rho_3F-\rho_4P-\rho_5S_f-\rho_6R_{comp}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-9b9b-c31ad6af6577" class="">Q=\frac{1}{T_f}(1-\pi_{rev})\frac{1}{1+\sigma_f}<br/>\quad;\quad<br/>\Phi=w_L\ln(1+L)+w_D\ln(1+D)+w_C\ln(1+C)+w_I\ln(1+I)-w_PP-w_GG-w_{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{E}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-8f3b-c1e45ef00ef2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_f=\mathcal{E}+K+\mathrm{LegacyLock}+\mathrm{CoordinationCost}
\quad;\quad
\mathcal{E}=\alpha K+\beta H+\gamma(1-F)+\delta\,\mathrm{Ambiguity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-bbab-fbdd67daddef" class="">Shared primitives (carry-over)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-886e-e5d90196317e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or reduce to <strong>10 irreducible axioms</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-a39b-d00a73266fa9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or formalize as a <strong>publishable unified dominance theorem</strong>,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-84af-e3b1f290e249" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Collapse 1–900 into a <strong>single compact master equation system</strong>,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-88a4-cdf806826043" class="">If you want to escalate further, the only remaining step is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-b53e-dcf94eb64ec8" class="">You now have <strong>900 structural levers, invariants, and equations</strong> forming a complete multi-domain dominance architecture across economics, security, governance, control theory, entropy, reflexivity, and systemic risk.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-b656-fbb2c2e35e2a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-98c3-c73f07f29857" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Final closure</strong><br/>Dominance is a constrained, metastable, reflexive equilibrium sustained by invariant enforcement, entropy minimization, capture resistance, 
and bounded adaptability under macro stress.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-9f89-c5e151baccf9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists T:\forall t\in[0,T], x_t \in \mathcal{D} \wedge \rho(J_F)&lt;1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-811f-d2a3b41f50e8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Global dominance condition (ultimate form)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-9e94-ed4523bb4407" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Optimality corollary</strong><br/>Best achievable system minimizes risk under physical and political constraints.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-9f35-efbf658961fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL=0 \wedge MPL=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-a211-f7d42ccbbbb0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Zero-risk impossibility corollary</strong><br/>No system satisfies:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-bdaa-c22c2b3998c1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_X &gt; U_{BTC} \wedge \Phi_{BTC} \text{ stable}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-8e69-f9f9f6a64bf5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Coexistence theorem</strong><br/>Utility rail and reserve asset can coexist if:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-b1b7-d0787f3d3210" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL\uparrow \wedge MPL\uparrow \wedge P\uparrow \wedge S_f\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-b675-d508f17a5481" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance break theorem</strong><br/>Break occurs when:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-ade4-e85cb62f949f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Dominance persistence theorem</strong><br/>Persistence requires bounded drift + bounded risk + positive reflexivity.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-a377-d3623ee7f874" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Meta-dominance theorem</strong><br/>A system dominates when it optimizes multi-axis risk-adjusted utility.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-89c7-cc3696bbf7bf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta\theta \le \delta_\theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-8c90-d0106e1e2df8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Adaptability constraint</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-8f5d-e48d3eff018e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min G \text{ subject to adaptability}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-9722-c7c842276c3d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Capture resistance principle</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-94c2-d20b7c597168" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min \mathcal{E} \text{ subject to bounded risk}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-b2a2-ea707f4d6a2f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Entropy minimization principle</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-a55b-d16f57fdd2e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I\uparrow \Rightarrow C\uparrow \Rightarrow L\uparrow \Rightarrow \Phi\uparrow \Rightarrow E\uparrow \Rightarrow U\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-9393-c75467e6040b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Insurance–liquidity–collateral flywheel</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-afc3-e8b6b2604bf5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}\in \mathcal{D} \text{ even when } Z=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-b449-f7d668d73af1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Crisis robustness condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-bb39-f173a7e7d427" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(J_F)&lt;1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-9fbe-f82a0d9d1648" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Stability condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-9b14-d133c15e3c15" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t \in \mathcal{D},\ \forall t \in [0,T]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-a2a1-d07ae21b33cc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Invariant enforcement condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-b751-c9b427b9c200" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{D} = \{x: U\uparrow,\Phi\uparrow,S_f\downarrow,EL\le\epsilon,MPL\le\Lambda,P\le P_{max}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-a642-f8a37851629a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance region</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-816e-c7287c12dfa2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=F(x_t,u_t,Z_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-a6e5-c2e526bdade3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>System evolution</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-a5a9-d29fd052b604" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Z = \{\text{macro},\text{political},\text{technical},\text{liquidity}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-9637-e30324c88630" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Shock vector</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-86d0-d248edc1e57e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u = \{\tau_g,q_g,\delta_\theta,FeePolicy,OperatorRules\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-9477-c930208ba812" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Control vector</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-a7ab-eac094d8d9a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x = \{U,\Phi,S_f,EL,MPL,P,L,D,C,\mathcal{E}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-92fa-f0246147bc04" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>State vector definition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-9573-fc222df0c0dd" class="">881–900: Grand Unified Closure</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-97d5-fead093fa500"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-a190-c2398c94e667" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Perfect dominance impossible; stable dominance achievable</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-93ba-fc911c1bc2d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU}{dt} \to 0,\ \frac{d\Phi}{dt}\to 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-9b32-d7cfc4133131" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Asymptotic dominance equilibrium condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-8645-e72471467551" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_t \|\Delta x_t\| &lt; \Theta_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-b09b-d5cc3e44b701" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Long-horizon stability requires bounded drift</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-b4f7-c367c1323a1a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Dominance decay requires cumulative invariant violations</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-8e7b-ecef0258220a" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Metastable dominance persists until multi-variable shock</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-8e88-c5f527e32579" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial \Omega_{replace}}{\partial t} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-82da-c859cb3987ed" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Replacement probability decreases over time</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-b168-c0cd26510dcd" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Marginal gains diminish near saturation</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-bd68-d1cc93add0d2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U \le U_{global}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-9d07-f772977fe91b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Adoption saturation limit</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-9f1d-de27ff84f4b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L \le L_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-a9d4-e73b3cb284ec" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liquidity bounded by capital base</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-9a4a-d66e797bab11" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C \le C_{global}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-8d37-d312f04af656" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Collateral acceptance finite</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-84aa-fc9bf013d193" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I \le I_{market}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-a831-f9551cb3f046" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Insurance capacity finite</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-ae12-dab5ac788efd" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Entropy never fully eliminated</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-9151-e7d336245b74" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Political risk never zero</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-9d5d-f7d9812da1fa" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Information asymmetry never zero</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8041-9836-efda213f6cff" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Optimal system minimizes risk under constraints</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-93db-d409c7a25aa6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MPL &gt; 0 \text{ always}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e1-8f4d-ce8740de3e85" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Zero risk unattainable</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-be4d-cd0a6b2d8a53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EL &gt; 0 \text{ always}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-ac75-ed488d6c46ae" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Perfect security impossible</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-890d-d8019b7d6d0d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_g \ge \tau_{human}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-bde9-dd09deebb784" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Governance reaction lower bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-a6f2-d248bc64ff58" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_f \ge T_{network}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-89f3-c8ff9d47196f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Latency lower bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-9d1a-e5ee1589b48b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Th \le Th_{physical}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-80d4-eb93cb33fde3" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Throughput upper bound</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-9448-c9b7fde54d38" class="">861–880: Asymptotic Limits &amp; Upper Bounds</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-9b4b-c5dfeecf5345"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-b429-e666c42d40ff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta U \propto \Delta \Phi - \Delta S_f - \Delta P</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-ba99-defad4fad408" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Equilibrium shift equation</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-a013-ea361a3df54b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Replacement requires exogenous shock OR superior risk profile</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-b43f-d7d6b77b0a9d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Inertia increases replacement threshold</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-aa8b-e50ee1ce7696" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Embeddedness increases inertia</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-b782-de50ac50bed9" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Standard adoption increases embeddedness</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-8474-f7949b021a47" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Meta-dominance achieved when new axes become standards</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-ab6d-cd3735bddb42" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Axis clarity reduces cognitive entropy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-9200-db9850aa727d" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>Market education reduces axis ambiguity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-b4b0-ef30e722bf10" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li><strong>Axis persistence increases inertia</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" i
d="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-903e-d00252640180" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li><strong>Hybrid strategy targets institutional axes</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-9982-f0b03cf88c49" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li><strong>Retail buyers weigh narrative axes higher</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-99a5-dfe2d44800d8" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="12"><li><strong>Institutional buyers weigh risk axes higher</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-abd8-c2d5a68523b3" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="13"><li><strong>Procurement gates act as axis weights</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-87a1-d205f4086fb2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_i w_i (Z_i^X - Z_i^{BTC}) &gt; \Theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-bae6-d5a4c5d1c336" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Threshold crossing condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-909f-e09e2c464cf8" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Single-axis superiority insufficient</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-b34b-d76b08724144" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Replacement requires multi-axis superiority</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-ab76-d0d4d049026c" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Category creation reduces direct comparison</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-a38b-dcfc8b6840bf" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Axis redefinition changes equilibrium</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-b5ab-f87230635b46" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Score}_X(Z) &gt; \mathrm{Score}_{BTC}(Z)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803d-81fa-c6546f05d36d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Axis dominance condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-9742-dadaee423bcb" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Competition occurs on evaluation axes</strong><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-a9de-eead325ff80c" class="">Define axis vector .</p></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-bd3e-feec46d69d96" class="">841–860: Meta-Competition &amp; System Replacement Theory</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-9413-e5291aefa0e5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-8531-ca34e3403544" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Entropy boundedness is a dominance requirement</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-ae67-d24928e002ec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial C}{\partial \mathcal{E}} &lt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-8ad2-e1316fe91509" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Entropy–collateral inverse relation</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-9bcf-c4173341503f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial L}{\partial \mathcal{E}} &lt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-96c2-df7d31b8bbc7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Entropy–liquidity inverse relation</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-989f-d98fcc925870" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Stress-resilient systems cap entropy injection</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-a053-dae4b20cc1a6" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Stress increases entropy injection rate</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-9f59-f43e04021ae7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d\mathcal{E}}{dt}=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-a322-c718833c31c7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Entropy equilibrium condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-acd3-f833aeb49bea" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Amendment cap reduces long-term entropy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-8488-f1222285df62" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Entropy accumulates via unbounded amendments</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-aed0-fdb2b9c44fe3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E} + EL + MPL</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-84da-c0522306e6c0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Optimal governance minimizes:</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-bd8f-fe6c3e37a1ae" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Excess rigidity increases operational loss</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-aa8d-d2579bef261c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Excess flexibility increases entropy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-9c12-e4da9b49e133" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Minimum entropy configuration defines optimal governance</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-9ff0-c653db4cd9fa" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Complexity–entropy tradeoff curve exists</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-974b-ec57125e2be9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E} \uparrow \text{ with Ambiguity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-b119-eb2f0ee37a46" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Entropy leak through policy ambiguity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-95a8-e47252854e68" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E} \uparrow \text{ with fragmentation}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-b914-d3e07a4cc15e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fragmentation increases entropy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-94d4-d81e0d299543" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E} \downarrow \text{ with standards adoption}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-9f63-e45a7c34896f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Standardization reduces entropy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-b249-e2c10f9ebdba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial MPL}{\partial \mathcal{E}} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-baf4-d3f0598b0e9f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Operational disorder increases MPL</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-990d-d5f488bbaa11" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Cost \propto \mathcal{E}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-90c6-d1a8522354cc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Governance friction converts entropy into cost</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-8c26-cba3095ec1f4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d\mathcal{E}}{dt} \le 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-8dcd-e42845f86de1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Invariant enforcement reduces entropy growth</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-91b9-fab315e62514" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d\mathcal{E}}{dt} &gt; 0 \text{ if no invariant enforcement}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-a98c-e8c1df925a37" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Institutional entropy increases without constraint</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-8c19-d014dcfd605f" class="">821–840: Institutional Thermodynamics</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-a76e-eaea541789d1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-a0b4-e1770fea31f8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Controlled reflexivity creates stable growth</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-952d-f46ab0c9f426" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Unbounded reflexivity creates bubbles</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-b1ff-c385cd64ecde" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Reflexivity damping requires invariant enforcement</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-8e20-cffbe2bcfc1b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dU}{dt} \to 0 \text{ at equilibrium}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-b58e-e90892deb978" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance plateau condition</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-a0e1-d9739ea95c26" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U &lt; U_{crit} \Rightarrow \text{migration accelerates}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-aae4-e7bd2b356850" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Switching cascade threshold</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-97e4-c12ac7749d94" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_{replace} \uparrow \text{ as } E \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-9ca4-db9a02572adf" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Embeddedness inertia</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800e-9d92-ff7d7dc73df1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial E}{\partial U} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-9d94-cfd57ca18fb8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Embeddedness feedback</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-a268-eb2064e8167e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\kappa_D = \frac{\partial U}{\partial \Phi}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-a764-d14aba5e5dba" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dominance reinforcement constant</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-ba8c-ffef36a5cc81" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dLeg}{dt} \propto A_{audit} + I - Incidents</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-9172-c751ee395600" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Trust compounding rate</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-a486-c9fc3cb73ddc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dN}{dt} = r_{rec} (N_{max} - N)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-b6c3-efbb4e970f2f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Recovery velocity parameter</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-b6a1-d65e13cef360" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N(t) = N_0 e^{-\lambda t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-9d85-fed546606cfb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Confidence half-life</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-9890-f526a97c43a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L &lt; L_{crit} \Rightarrow \frac{dL}{dt} &lt; 0 \text{ accelerates}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-b942-d050a4c12773" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liquidity evaporation threshold</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-9cb7-ed58257fa61e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta N_{crisis} \propto \mathrm{IncidentSeverity}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-a446-fde5d46d20bd" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Crisis reflexivity multiplier</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-b748-cb37f5f66013" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_r &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-b964-fce9c0778fb7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Stability requires loop gain &lt; 1</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-b934-e00b25312a23" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Runaway boom/bust}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-9711-fca2abdb96b1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Reflexive instability condition</strong><br/>If loop gain :</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-8b04-c7c8068c8563" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial C}{\partial \Phi} &gt; 0,\quad \frac{\partial \Phi}{\partial C} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-8ae1-d4ed204bca71" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Collateral reflexivity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-97b1-e9b33214f293" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial D}{\partial L} &gt; 0,\quad \frac{\partial L}{\partial D} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-98ce-c10bf30da339" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Derivative reflexivity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-ae12-f2071b618355" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial L}{\partial U} &gt; 0,\quad \frac{\partial U}{\partial L} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-aa1b-f4c3c886deea" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Liquidity reflexivity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-9774-fdda64f70df6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\eta_N = \frac{\partial U}{\partial N}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-9134-e9f3f3d109a6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Narrative elasticity coefficient</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-8c07-d49d82ae54fa" class="">\frac{dU}{dt} = h(N)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-98e2-e02dda12bdaf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dN}{dt} = f(U) - g(\text{incidents})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-a226-cbb5935984aa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Adoption changes perception, which changes adoption.</strong><br/>Reflexive loop:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-bec1-d246085b8408" class="">801–820: Reflexivity and Self-Reinforcing Dynamics</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-ad59-c3619981407f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-9f36-f0668687d076" class="">All tied to invariants or equations.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-abd3-ee448686b609" class="">These push into: reflexivity, second-order dominance, meta-competition, institutional thermodynamics, and asymptotic limits.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bd-97c5-dc97ac9cb777" class="">Below are <strong>801–900</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-930d-e7a5aebe7d84" class="">I cannot permanently store or “remember everything” beyond this session unless explicitly saved as structured memory. Within this conversation, all prior structure is active and being extended.</p></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
